*Shutong Wu,Kevin Calderone,Andy Tsen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CraftBenchUE，一个在隔离 Unreal Engine 环境中运行智能体、重建其提交并执行确定性构建、资产与运行时检查（不使用 LLM 评判）的评测框架，并据此构建 70 项任务基准，发现 C++ 完成率显著高于 Blueprint，且部分通过资产检查的 Blueprint 提交仍失败于运行时断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在游戏引擎中实现游戏玩法不仅需要能编译运行的代码，代码可编译运行并不代表真正实现了所要求的玩法，因此需要能验证实际运行行为的评测手段。

Method: 提出 CraftBenchUE 评测框架：在隔离 Unreal Engine 环境中运行智能体，将其保存的提交在新工程中重建，并施加确定性的构建、资产与运行时检查，全程不依赖 LLM 评判；基于该框架构建覆盖 C++ 源码、Blueprint 资产与编辑器脚本的 70 项任务基准；在两种编辑器工具配置下评测七个模型，并在 C++ 任务上设置文件与 shell 基线；此外设计 10 组指定相同玩法、使用相同运行时测试但分别要求 C++ 与 Blueprint 交付的配对任务。

Result: 在 10 组配对任务中，两种工具配置下 C++ 完成率分别比 Blueprint 高出 30.0 和 42.9 个百分点；在该配对集中按时提交且通过资产检查的 Blueprint 提交里，分别有 42.2% 和 50.0% 失败于显式运行时断言。

Conclusion: 部分提交虽满足资产要求，却未通过所要求的玩法测试，说明仅靠资产检查不足以判定玩法实现正确；作者将随报告发布评测框架、任务基准与轨迹发现。

Abstract: Building gameplay features in a game engine requires more than code, as code that compiles and runs does not necessarily implement the requested gameplay. We introduce CraftBenchUE, an evaluation harness that runs agents in an isolated Unreal Engine environment, reconstructs their saved submissions in fresh projects, and applies deterministic build, asset, and runtime checks without an LLM judge. Based on the harness, we built a benchmark consisting of 70 tasks spanning C++ source, Blueprint assets, and editor scripting. We evaluate seven models under two editor-tool configurations, with a file-and-shell baseline on C++ tasks. We further pair tasks that specify the same gameplay and use the same runtime tests, but require C++ and Blueprint as the deliverables. Across the 10 paired tasks, C++ completion rates exceed Blueprint by 30.0 and 42.9 percentage points in the two tool configurations. Among on-time Blueprint submissions in this paired set that pass asset checks, 42.2% and 50.0% fail explicit runtime assertions. These submissions satisfy asset requirements but fail the required gameplay tests. We will release the harness, task benchmark, and our trajectory findings with the report.

</details>


### [19] [Do Not Trust the Benchmark: Limitations of General LLM Rankings and a Case for Task-Specific Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23201)
*Danial Amin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统剖析通用大语言模型排行榜的五类局限，主张评估应公开被测配置、验证题目与任务完成、附带成本与耗时，并明确泛化范围；并以众包平台Isotanta为例，区分共享排名与任务特定/用户自建评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数日益影响大模型的开发、营销与选型，但总分只有在明确被测系统、题目集合与评测条件时才可解释；通用排行榜的单一排名易被误读为模型能力的普适结论，可能误导用户选型。

Method: 采用观点综述（perspective）方式，归纳并串联五类相互关联的局限：被测系统与公开可用系统的差异；外部评估中的商业激励与依赖；基准饱和、缺陷题目与数据污染；模型针对评分程序的投机性利用；通用分数与用户实际任务的相关性有限。结合已记录案例说明各类问题需要不同应对，并提出评估规范主张；随后以众包基准平台Isotanta作为实践案例进行讨论。

Result: 论证指出：更大的题目池可提升任务覆盖，重复采样可提升该题目池上估计的稳定性，但二者都不保证效度与个性化；Isotanta当前的共享排名与所提出的任务特定评估、用户自供评估需加以区分。

Conclusion: 模型选择需要关于目标工作实际表现的证据，而非通用排行榜上的高名次；评估流程应披露被测配置、验证题目与成功完成情况、同时报告性能与成本及执行时间，并明确泛化边界。

Abstract: Benchmark scores increasingly influence the development, marketing, and selection of large language models (LLMs). Yet an overall score is interpretable only in relation to the system tested, the questions included, and the conditions of evaluation. This perspective examines five connected limitations of general LLM rankings: differences between evaluated and publicly available systems; commercial incentives and dependencies in external evaluation; benchmark saturation, defective tests, and data contamination; models exploiting scoring procedures; and the limited relevance of general scores to users' tasks. Documented cases illustrate why these problems require different responses. I argue for evaluation procedures that disclose the tested configuration, validate questions and successful task completion, report performance alongside cost and execution time, and make the scope of generalization explicit. I then discuss \textbf{Isotanta}, a crowdsourced benchmarking platform, as a practical example of contributed questions and repeated evaluation. A larger question pool may improve task coverage, while repeated sampling can improve the stability of estimates on that pool; neither guarantees validity or personalization. The paper distinguishes the platform's current shared ranking from proposed task-specific and user-provided evaluations. Its central argument is that model selection requires evidence about performance on the intended work, not simply a high position on a general leaderboard.

</details>


### [20] [Divergent strategies and convergent outcomes in autonomous materials discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23957)
*Jihan Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究同一模型-框架配置在重复开放式科研任务中的表现差异：16个独立初始化的智能体在相同冻结数据库、目标与协议下，策略分歧明显，但结论高度一致，同时暴露了由共享输入导致的共性错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学智能体评估多关注任务完成度或已知结果复现，忽视了重复开放式研究中的变异性与共模错误。作者希望考察同一配置在多次独立运行中是否稳定、结论是否可复现，以及强制校验能否提升结论有效性。

Method: 设置16个独立初始化的会话（同一模型-框架配置），给予12,499个金属有机框架（MOF）的冻结数据库、甲烷存储目标、固定协议和一周预算；对其中一半智能体施加强制校验。随后对结果、策略差异、筛选规模、新构建结构及复现性进行对比分析，并独立计算数据库多孔区域作为参照。

Result: 策略分化为四类，筛选规模从100至5,000个结构不等，8个智能体构建了2,253个假设结构；但所有智能体均收敛到约200 cm³/cm³的同一材料前沿（materials frontier）。独立计算数据库多孔区域所得九个最佳结构均出现在智能体报告中。强制校验使新运行复现率从1/8提升至8/8，但未能显著改善结论有效性，因为16个智能体中15个选择了同一被审计排除项——一个因缺失阴离子而产生人工孔体积的不完整结构。

Conclusion: 重复运行的科学智能体既展现了结论的稳健性，也揭示了共享输入造成的共模错误；强制校验可提升复现率，却无法自动保证结论有效性，审计排除项被普遍选中即是例证。

Abstract: Scientific agents are mostly evaluated on whether they complete tasks or recover known results; we instead study variation across repeated open-ended campaigns. Sixteen separately initialized sessions of one model-harness configuration received a frozen database of 12,499 metal-organic frameworks, a methane-storage objective, a pinned protocol and a one-week budget. Strategies diverged into four approaches spanning 100--5,000 screened structures, and eight built 2,253 hypothetical structures. Yet the agents recovered the same materials frontier near 200 cm^3/cm^3, and an independent calculation of the database's porous region found its nine best structures all among their reports. Enforced checks on half the agents raised fresh-run reproduction from one of eight to eight of eight but could not detectably improve conclusion validity, because fifteen of sixteen agents selected the same audit-excluded entry, an incomplete structure whose missing anions created artificial pore volume. Replicated agents thus reveal both robust conclusions and common-mode errors from shared inputs.

</details>


### [21] [AgentBetta: Verification-Driven Adaptive Configuration of an AI Nano-Agent through Selective Expansion and Verified Contraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23512)
*Md. Ashraful Babu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了自适应Nano-Agent框架AgentBetta，将模型能力、上下文、工具、权限、记忆与算力等表示为可执行配置，并通过验证驱动的诊断、选择性扩展与反事实收缩进行更新，证明自适应配置能调节能力分配与推理开销，但并非专用智能体架构的通用替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体通常以预定义配置部署，但不同任务所需的模型能力、上下文、工具、权限、记忆和计算资源差异显著，固定满配会导致资源浪费与能力过度暴露。

Method: 提出自适应Nano-Agent框架AgentBetta，将配置因素表示为可执行配置，通过验证驱动诊断、选择性扩展和基于验证的反事实收缩进行更新；评估分为受控机制验证与外部智能体对比，并在AB-ConfigBench上测试。

Result: 在AB-ConfigBench上达到91.38%验证成功率，中位上下文分配由64,000降至8,000字符，中位工具暴露由5个降至0；配置缺陷诊断宏F1为0.819、精确率1.000；56.41%的单维收缩探针保持验证结果；外部评估显示跨基准与智能体族结果不一，跨族复现未重现主骨干准确率排序，专用系统在部分任务域仍具优势。

Conclusion: AgentBetta应被理解为一种调节能力分配与推理开销的配置适应机制，而非专用智能体架构的通用替代方案。

Abstract: Large language model agents are typically deployed with predefined configurations, although the required model capability, context, tools, permissions, memory, and computational resources can vary substantially across tasks. This study develops and evaluates AgentBetta, an adaptive AI Nano-Agent framework that represents these factors as an executable configuration and updates them through verification-driven diagnosis, selective expansion, and verification-based counterfactual contraction. The evaluation distinguishes controlled mechanism validation from external agent comparisons. On the AB-ConfigBench benchmark, AgentBetta achieved 91.38% verified success while reducing median context allocation from 64,000 to 8,000 context characters and median tool exposure from five tools to zero compared with the fully provisioned configuration. The configuration-deficiency diagnosis achieved a macro-F1 score of 0.819 with precision of 1.000 across the evaluated dimensions, and selective expansion avoided unnecessary changes to unrelated configuration dimensions. Post-success contraction preserved verification outcomes in 56.41% of evaluated one-dimension contraction probes, indicating that some successful configurations contained removable capability under the tested conditions. External evaluations indicate that adaptive configuration can improve the balance between verified task completion and capability exposure; however, the results vary across benchmarks and agent families. In particular, the cross-family replication did not reproduce the primary-backbone accuracy ordering, and specialized systems remained advantageous for certain task domains. These results support interpreting AgentBetta as a configuration-adaptation mechanism that regulates capability allocation and inference expenditure rather than as a universal replacement for specialized agent architectures.

</details>


### [22] [PhysAI-Bench: A Benchmark for LLM-Based Agentic Decision-Making in Autonomous UAV-Centric Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23695)
*Mohamed Amine Ferrag,Merouane Debbah,Abderrahmane Lakas,Manu Perumkunnil,Norbert Tihanyi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 PhysAI-Bench，一个用于评估自主无人机任务中智能体决策能力的基础模型基准，包含 10,178 个标准化决策实例，覆盖 29 个基础模型，最高准确率仅 52.00%，表明 Physical AI 的可靠智能体决策仍是开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Physical AI 推动了基础模型在无人机等自主系统中的应用，这些系统需在动态环境中完成感知、推理、规划与行动。然而现有基准多评估物理感知、直觉物理、具身导航与协作推理，鲜有评估可靠自主性所需的智能体决策能力，因此需要专门的评估基准。

Method: 构建 PhysAI-Bench，从自主无人机任务的对话轨迹中自动抽取 10,178 个标准化决策实例，每个实例保留任务上下文、时序依赖、物理约束、MCP 工具调用、A2A 交互、传感器观测及 AI 原生 6G 网络条件（延迟、丢包、吞吐、边缘负载、网络切片）。仅暴露决策前的信息以防止未来事件泄漏，近似在线决策。采用两阶段协议评估 29 个基础模型：先在 35 个经人工验证的开发集上，从 zero/three/five-shot 与四种温度共 12 种组合中选出模型特定配置，各测三次；再冻结配置，在固定的、按 episode 不相交的 500 个实例上各测三次。

Result: GPT-5.3 准确率最高（52.00%），其次为 GPT-5.2（49.40%）和 Grok 4.5（49.07%）。少样本提示通常提升性能，温度影响有限。总体结果显示基础模型在该任务上表现接近随机水平，可靠智能体决策仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: PhysAI-Bench 填补了 Physical AI 中智能体决策评估的空白，通过防泄漏的在线决策设定与标准化两阶段评估协议，揭示了当前基础模型在可靠自主决策方面的显著不足，表明该问题仍是开放挑战。数据集已公开于 GitHub。

Abstract: Recent advances in Physical AI have accelerated the use of foundation models in autonomous systems such as unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), which must perceive, reason, plan, and act in dynamic environments. Existing benchmarks assess physical perception, intuitive physics, embodied navigation, and collaborative reasoning, but rarely evaluate the agentic decision-making required for reliable autonomy. We introduce \textit{PhysAI-Bench}, a benchmark for evaluating this capability. It contains 10,178 standardized decision instances automatically extracted from conversational traces of autonomous UAV missions. Each instance preserves mission context, temporal dependencies, physical constraints, Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool calls, Agent-to-Agent (A2A) interactions, sensor observations, and AI-native 6G network conditions, including latency, packet loss, throughput, edge load, and network slicing. We expose only information preceding each decision, preventing future-event leakage and approximating online decision-making. We evaluate 29 foundation models using a two-stage protocol. We select model-specific configurations from 12 combinations of zero-, three-, and five-shot prompting and four temperatures, tested in three runs on a 35-instance, human-verified development set. We then freeze each selected configuration and evaluate it in three runs on a fixed, episode-disjoint set of 500 instances. GPT-5.3 achieves the highest accuracy (52.00%), followed by GPT-5.2 (49.40%) and Grok~4.5 (49.07%). Few-shot prompting generally improves performance, while temperature has limited influence. The results demonstrate that reliable agentic decision-making in Physical AI remains an open challenge. The dataset is available at https://github.com/maferrag/physai-bench

</details>


### [23] [Total Cost of Agency: Exact Attribution of Memory Injection Cost in Multi-Agent LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23790)
*Vivek Kumar Singh,Preeti Priyam,Gautam Bhowmick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"代理总成本"(TCA),将多智能体LLM工作流成本分解为基础的prompt、推理、记忆注入、未命中惩罚与上下文累积五部分,并用两遍非计费token计数精确归因;实测显示记忆注入占可变成本13.6%、全部计费成本约12%,且随深度从0升至27.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体LLM工作流中,每个节点从记忆中检索上下文并注入prompt,这些注入token按与系统提示和用户查询相同的单价计费,但生产可观测性工具只报告总token成本,不区分节点生成的token与被动接收的token,导致这部分成本对付费团队完全不可见。作者希望使该成本组成部分可见、可归因、可控。

Method: 提出Total Cost of Agency(TCA),将多智能体工作流成本分解为基础prompt、推理、记忆注入、miss penalty与context-accumulation五部分;并给出精确归因方法:两遍(two-pass)非计费token计数,直接测量注入token而非用词数代理估计。在200任务企业基准上调用真实模型API评测;并做控制实验:将检索窗口容量从32降到2。

Result: 记忆注入占编译期优化器可作用的可变成本的13.6%,约占全部计费成本的12%;其占比从工作流深度1的结构性零升至深度6的27.6%。在深度2至6范围内,注入token随深度线性增长(R²=0.9974);二次拟合的二次项系数为负,故该深度范围未出现凸性增长。固定模型档位时该分量可控:检索窗口容量32→2使注入token减少28.7%,准确率变化在种子级波动范围内。作者同时如实报告:图重写变换单独看近似成本中性;五个分解项中两项在此测试框架下按构造为零;总工作流成本由模型档位分配主导(该因素被固定并视为先前工作)。未评估prompt caching,所有数据均为未缓存情形。

Conclusion: 记忆注入是此前不可见但可测量的成本组成部分,可通过检索窗口容量等固定模型档位下的手段加以控制,而TCA与两遍精确归因方法使其可被量化。作者强调结果的边界:图重写变换近似成本中性、两项分解项按构造为零、总成本由模型档位主导、未评估缓存。

Abstract: Every node in a multi-agent large language model (LLM) workflow retrieves context from memory and injects it into its prompt, where those injected tokens are billed as input tokens at the same per-token price as the system prompt and the user query. Production observability tools report total token cost but do not separate the tokens a node generates from the tokens it is handed, so this component of the bill is invisible to the teams paying it. We introduce the Total Cost of Agency (TCA), a decomposition of multi-agent workflow cost into base prompt, inference, memory injection, miss penalty and context-accumulation components, and an exact attribution method: a two-pass, non-billable token count that measures injected tokens directly rather than estimating them from word-count proxies. On a 200-task enterprise benchmark executed against real model APIs, memory injection accounts for 13.6 percent of the variable cost a compile-time optimizer can act on, about 12 percent of the full billed cost, and its share rises from a structural zero at workflow depth one to 27.6 percent at depth six. Injected tokens grow linearly with depth over the measured range (R^2 = 0.9974, depths two through six); a quadratic fit yields a negative leading coefficient, so the data do not exhibit convex growth at these depths. We show the component is controllable at fixed model tier: reducing the retrieval window capacity from 32 to 2 entries lowers injected tokens by 28.7 percent with an accuracy change within seed-level variation. We report in full that our graph-rewriting transforms are approximately cost-neutral in isolation, that two of the five decomposition terms are zero by construction in this harness, and that total workflow cost is dominated by model tier assignment, which we hold fixed and treat as prior work. Prompt caching is not evaluated; all figures are for the uncached case.

</details>


### [24] [Pretraining of Medical Visual Encoders Toward Multi-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23860)
*Tianyou Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出MedMLIP框架，通过报告生成任务用冻结LLM预训练视觉编码器，缓解MLLM中CLIP视觉编码器与自回归LLM之间的语义接口不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型常复用CLIP预训练的视觉编码器，但其ViT特征最终由自回归LLM消费，二者之间存在语义接口不匹配（semantic-interface gap）。

Method: 提出MedMLIP框架：借助报告生成任务、以冻结LLM指导预训练视觉编码器，并采用局部关系蒸馏（LRD）保持视觉patch间关系以避免视觉坍塌；在IU-Xray和Open-PMC-300K上预训练，仅迁移ViT、替换引导LLM与投影器以评估跨LLM迁移性。

Result: 在VQA-RAD和SLAKE上评估所得编码器，跨LLM迁移实验证明了为自回归LLM接口预训练视觉编码器的价值，同时尽可能保留更细粒度视觉信息。

Conclusion: 面向自回归LLM接口预训练视觉编码器并保留细粒度视觉信息具有实际价值，代码与预训练模型已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) commonly reuse visual encoders pretrained with CLIP, although the features of these ViTs are ultimately consumed by autoregressive LLMs. We refer to this mismatch as the semantic-interface gap and introduce MedMLIP, a framework that pretrains the visual encoder through report generation with a frozen LLM, while employing Local Relational Distillation (LRD) to preserve relationships among visual patches to avoid visual collapse. We pretrain MedMLIP on IU-Xray and Open-PMC-300K and evaluate the resulting encoders on VQA-RAD and SLAKE. Only the ViT is transferred, while the guiding LLM and projector are replaced, allowing us to assess cross-LLM transferability. Our cross-LLM transfer experiments demonstrate the value of pretraining visual encoders for their autoregressive LLM interface while trying to preserve more fine-grained visual information. Code and the pretrained model are available at https://github.com/SkyCol/MedMLIP

</details>


### [25] [Explainable Recommendations at Scale: LLM Rationales for YouTube Music Artist Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23877)
*Xiao Liu,Yanwei Song,Srivaths Ranganathan,Yuan Chen,Zheyun Feng,Parker Steenburgh,Jochen Klingenhoefer,Nathan Lasche,Gergo Varady,Tim Steele*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出解耦式推荐架构，将LLM推理异步离线化，预计算未发现艺人的个性化候选池及定制化推荐理由，在低延迟约束下规模化提升音乐推荐探索性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐流媒体平台面临"利用熟悉内容"与"推动探索新内容"的权衡：用户虽渴望发现新内容，却不愿选择陌生艺人。透明、自然语言的推荐理由可降低这一信任门槛，但LLM虽擅长此类可解释性生成，其高昂的推理成本与计算开销使实时部署严重受阻。

Method: 工业界案例研究：设计解耦式推荐架构，将LLM推理异步隔离至离线阶段，预先计算由未发现艺人构成的个性化候选池，并同步生成定制化推荐理由，从而将LLM能力与在线服务延迟解耦。

Result: 大规模在线A/B实验验证了该设计的有效性：LLM驱动的推荐结合解释性理由显著降低了新内容的信任门槛，在发现场景下的用户探索度与整体参与度均取得统计显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM推理离线化并与在线服务解耦，可在不牺牲延迟的前提下规模化实现可解释的探索性推荐；自然语言推荐理由对缓解新内容信任壁垒具有实证有效性。

Abstract: Modern music streaming platforms face a persistent tradeoff: exploiting familiar content versus driving the exploration of novel items. While users frequently desire discovery, they hesitate to select unknown artists over proven favorites. Providing transparent, natural language rationales that explain why an unexplored item is recommended lowers this barrier. However, while Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at this nuanced explainability, their real-time deployment is severely bottlenecked by prohibitive inference costs and computational overhead. In this paper, we present an industry case study of a decoupled recommendation architecture that successfully scales exploration without compromising latency. Our system isolates LLM inference asynchronously offline, pre-computing personalized candidate pools of undiscovered artists alongside tailored rationales. Large-scale online A/B experiments validate our design. We demonstrate that combining LLM-backed recommendations with these explanatory rationales significantly reduces the trust barrier for new content, yielding statistically significant improvements in both user exploration and overall engagement on the discovery surfaces.

</details>


### [26] [Jev-Mem: System-One-Controlled Agentic Memory for Efficient AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23986)
*Dongming Jiang,Yi Li,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Jev-Mem，一种受双系统认知启发的智能体记忆架构，通过 System-One 控制平面主导记忆构建与检索决策，System-Two 仅负责复杂推理与答案合成，在 LoCoMo 上同时提升效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆系统多依赖自回归 LLM 控制记忆的组织、检索与使用，导致昂贵的生成过程处于记忆操作的关键路径上，成为长时程智能体的效率瓶颈。

Method: 借鉴 System-One/System-Two 认知划分，设计三层结构：System-One 控制平面在构建阶段管理记忆类型与关系组织，在检索阶段动态执行查询路由、检索预算分配、图遍历、候选打分与自适应停止；多关系记忆平面提供结构化存储；System-Two 推理平面仅在复杂推理与答案合成时被调用。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 基准上，LLM-as-a-Judge 总体得分 0.777，较最强基线相对提升 11.0%；记忆构建时间降至 158 秒，较最快竞品加速 6.6 倍；平均查询延迟降至 0.93 秒，降低 36.7%。

Conclusion: 将记忆控制权从生成式 LLM 转移到轻量 System-One 控制器，可在不牺牲记忆有效性的前提下显著提升系统效率，为长时程智能体记忆提供了一种效果与效率兼得的设计范式。

Abstract: Agentic memory is becoming essential for long-horizon AI agents, yet many existing systems rely on autoregressive LLMs to control how memories are organized, retrieved, and used, placing expensive generation on the critical path of memory operations. We introduce \textbf{\method}, a new agentic memory architecture inspired by System-One/System-Two cognition. System One captures fast, lightweight decision-making, whereas System Two performs slower, deliberative reasoning. Jev-Mem brings this division of labor to agentic memory through a dedicated System-One control plane, a structured multi-relational memory plane, and a System-Two reasoning plane. The System-One controller governs memory typing and relational organization during construction, and dynamically performs query routing, retrieval-budget allocation, graph traversal, candidate scoring, and adaptive stopping during retrieval. System Two is invoked only for complex reasoning and answer synthesis. This design improves both memory effectiveness and system efficiency: on LoCoMo Jev-Mem achieves an overall LLM-as-a-Judge score of 0.777, an 11.0\% relative improvement over the strongest baseline, while reducing memory construction time to 158\,s, a 6.6$\times$ speedup over the fastest competing memory system, and lowering average query latency to 0.93\,s, a 36.7\% reduction.

</details>


### [27] [FinInteract: Benchmarking Clarification and Intent Integration in Ambiguous Financial Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24002)
*Xinyu Wang,Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Zhenghan Tai,Guang Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 FinInteract 双语基准（173 个实例），用于衡量大模型金融问答智能体在问题表述不完备时主动澄清歧义并整合澄清信息的能力，揭示了"单一标准答案幻觉"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融监管文件中同一指标存在多种合法但不同的解读（如 Meta 合并口径与分部口径的 operating income），现有基准每题仅给一个标准答案，无法区分智能体是真正消解了歧义还是猜中了常见解读，形成"单一标准答案幻觉"这一评测盲区。

Method: 构建 FinInteract 基准，含英/中双语共 173 个实例，基于五类歧义分类体系，为每题同时提供默认解读与目标解读，并评分智能体是否能引出正确澄清并将其整合到答案中。

Result: 将相同输出对照默认解读而非目标解读重新评分，GPT-4o 准确率被虚高 3.1 倍，证实幻觉存在；补充解读后模型准确率超 90%，而需自行引出澄清时最高仅 28.9%；分类体系中实体范围与指标定义类别统计功效充足，其余类别为探索性；以歧义类别为条件可提升推理与训练阶段的消歧效果。

Conclusion: 单一标准答案的金融基准系统性高估了智能体的真实能力，主动澄清能力仍是显著短板；FinInteract 提供了可诊断该能力并支持分类体系条件化改进的评测工具。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly answer financial questions by searching regulatory filings. Such questions are often deceptively under-specified: Meta Platforms' "operating income" is $46.75B consolidated but $62.87B for the Family of Apps segment, and each reading is exactly verifiable against the filing. A capable agent should recognize the ambiguity and ask, rather than commit to a plausible but unintended reading. Existing financial benchmarks cannot measure this, because one gold answer per question cannot separate agents that resolve the ambiguity from those that guess the common reading, a blind spot we call the single-gold illusion. We release FinInteract, a bilingual (English/Chinese) benchmark of 173 instances that pairs each question with a default and an intended interpretation across a five-category ambiguity taxonomy, and grades whether an agent elicits the right clarification and then integrates it. Re-grading identical outputs against the default rather than the intended reading inflates GPT-4o's accuracy by 3.1 times, confirming the illusion. Beyond it, we find that models answer above 90% once the interpretation is supplied but at most 28.9% when they must elicit it themselves, that targeting is uneven across a taxonomy well powered for entity scope and metric definition and exploratory elsewhere, and that conditioning on the ambiguity category improves resolution at both inference and training time.

</details>


### [28] [Structured Decomposition for Reliable LLM-Generated Access Control Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24036)
*Vatsal Gupta,Darshan Sreenivasamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的端到端系统，将自然语言访问控制策略（NLACP）翻译为可执行的 Rego 代码（用于 OPA），通过模块化流程（策略检测、组件抽取、模式验证、linting、编译、自动测试生成与执行）弥合人类可读访问需求与策略即代码（PaC）之间的鸿沟，并在 372 条 ACRE 语句上验证其相对单提示基线的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可读的访问控制需求与机器可执行的策略即代码（PaC）之间存在语义鸿沟；直接使用单提示 LLM 生成授权策略可靠性低、难以部署，尤其在拒绝语义、属主条件和状态限定等安全关键模式上错误率高，因此需要一种结构化、可验证的生成方法。

Method: 构建模块化端到端流水线，将 NLACP 转换为 OPA 可执行的 Rego 代码，涵盖策略检测、组件抽取、模式（schema）验证、linting、编译以及自动化测试生成与执行；采用结构化分解（structured decomposition）与模式感知验证（schema-aware validation）两大核心机制。在 372 条具备非空 subject、action、resource 标注的 ACRE-complete 访问控制语句上进行评测，并与直接单提示 LLM 基线对比，以隔离结构化分解与模式验证的贡献。

Result: 端到端策略正确率达 50.3%，基线为 15.3%，提升 3.3 倍；正确性判定标准严格，须同时通过编译、linting 以及正向与负向测试。安全关键模式上：拒绝策略正确语义生成率 87.5%（基线 37.5%）；属主限定策略的条件生成率 100%（基线 40%）；状态限定策略的条件生成率 100%（基线 55.6%）。

Conclusion: 结构化分解与模式感知验证在提升 LLM 生成授权策略的可靠性方面起关键作用，能够显著改善安全关键模式的正确性，表明该流水线是可部署策略即代码生成的有效途径；端到端绝对正确率仍约半数，尚存进一步优化空间。

Abstract: This paper presents an LLM-based system that translates natural-language access control policies (NLACPs) into executable Rego code for Open Policy Agent (OPA). It provides a modular, end-to-end pipeline for policy detection, component extraction, schema validation, linting, compilation, and automated test generation and execution. The system is designed to bridge the gap between human-readable access requirements and machine-enforceable policy-as-code (PaC), with a focus on deployment reliability and security correctness.
  We evaluate the system on 372 ACRE-complete access control statements with non-null subject, action, and resource annotations against a direct single-prompt LLM baseline to isolate the contribution of structured decomposition and schema-aware validation. The system achieves a 50.3% end-to-end policy correctness rate, compared with 15.3% for the baseline, representing a 3.3x improvement. A policy is counted as correct only if it satisfies compilation, linting, and both positive and negative tests, making this a strict measure of deployable correctness.
  On security-critical patterns, the system generates correct deny semantics for 87.5% of deny policies (baseline: 37.5%), ownership conditions for 100% of ownership-qualified policies (baseline: 40%), and status-qualified conditions for 100% of status-qualified policies (baseline: 55.6%). These results indicate that structured decomposition and schema-aware validation play a critical role in improving the reliability of LLM-generated authorization policies.

</details>


### [29] [Incremental Consistency Execution for Autonomous Intelligent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24090)
*Cheng Li,Jiexiong Liu,Yixuan Chen,Ziheng Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于任务事实契约、字段级依赖掩码与状态扰动结果不变域的增量一致性执行方法，用于长时程异构智能体系统，在外部状态变化时避免全流程重执行，仅重算最小受影响字段并以等价屏障阻断无效传播，实验显示显著降低昂贵组件调用与端到端延迟，同时保持高一致性与低错误复用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程自主智能系统依赖大语言模型、数据库、外部API与规则引擎等异构组件，且执行期间外部状态持续变化。若每次变化都重新执行整个工作流，会产生大量冗余计算，代价高昂且时延大。因此需要一种在状态变化下保证一致性的增量执行机制。

Method: 方法由四部分构成：(1) 任务事实契约（task fact contracts）用于刻画任务执行所依赖的事实与约束；(2) 字段级依赖掩码（field-level dependency masks）精确描述输出字段与输入之间的依赖关系；(3) 状态扰动结果不变域（state perturbation result invariant domains）：在初次验证执行后，为关键输入构造保守的不变域，据此判定下游结果能否安全续用而无需重新调用昂贵组件；(4) 当必须重执行时，仅重算最小受影响输出字段，并通过等价屏障（equivalence barrier）阻止不必要的下游传播；此外引入提交时版本一致性门（submission-time version consistency gate）保障副作用动作的安全性。

Result: 在工业故障诊断、企业分析与基于LLM的多工具助手三类场景上的实验表明，该方法显著减少了昂贵组件的调用次数与端到端延迟，同时维持高一致性水平与较低的错误复用率。

Conclusion: 通过契约、依赖掩码与不变域驱动的增量一致性执行，可在状态动态变化的条件下安全复用既有结果、最小化重算范围，从而在异构长时程智能系统中实现效率与一致性的有效平衡。

Abstract: Long-horizon autonomous intelligent systems rely on heterogeneous components such as large language models, databases, external APIs, and rule engines, while their external states continuously change during execution. Re-executing the entire workflow after every change introduces substantial redundant computation. This paper proposes an incremental consistency execution method based on task fact contracts, field-level dependency masks, and state perturbation result invariant domains. After an initial verified execution, the system constructs conservative invariant domains for critical inputs and uses them to determine whether downstream results can be safely renewed without re-invoking expensive components. When re-execution is required, only the smallest affected output fields are recomputed, and an equivalence barrier prevents unnecessary downstream propagation. A submission-time version consistency gate further ensures the safety of side-effecting actions. Experiments on industrial fault diagnosis, enterprise analytics, and LLM-based multi-tool assistants show that the proposed method significantly reduces expensive component calls and end-to-end latency while maintaining high consistency and low incorrect-reuse rates.

</details>


### [30] [EDGEGEN: Improving Tool-Calling Agents Beyond Happy Paths with Synthetic Edge Case Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24115)
*Harshavardhan Abichandani,Penny Chong,Jiyuan Shen,Gunraj Singh,Ashutosh Hathidara,Marcus Duigan Xing Yu,Jane Lo,Atin Ghosh,Yipeng Li,Daniel Dahlmeier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 EdgeGen，从智能体规范中抽取合规规则，生成基于数据库的违规边界任务，实现无需人工标注的自动化闭环，用于微调与 harness 优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级工具调用 LLM 智能体需要高质量、多样化的任务数据集来评估与优化，但受隐私等约束难以获取。已有合成任务生成方法常产出通用任务，忽略智能体底层状态或数据库，且无法反映真实使用中的多样性。

Method: EdgeGen 从智能体规范中提取合规规则，并据此生成以数据库为依据、旨在违反这些规则的边界案例任务；再与既有合成数据生成技术结合，通过微调与 harness 优化提升智能体，形成全自动闭环流程，无需人工标注。

Result: 在 tau2bench airline 域上，基于 EdgeGen 生成数据微调可获得一致的均值进度提升（2% 至 42%），而其他基线方法在部分模型上出现性能退化；在 Gemma-4-e4b 模型上，harness 优化相对人工整理与基础 harness 分别取得约 10% 与 30% 的均值进度提升。

Conclusion: EdgeGen 通过规范驱动的合规规则与数据库落地的边界任务生成，弥补了现有合成任务通用化、脱离底层状态的不足；结合微调与 harness 优化可构建无需人工标注的闭环系统，在 tau2bench airline 域取得稳定提升。

Abstract: Tool-calling LLM agents are increasingly deployed in enterprise applications. However, effective evaluation and optimization require high-quality, diverse task datasets that are often difficult to obtain due to privacy and other constraints. Existing synthetic task generation methods often produce generic tasks that ignore an agent's underlying state or database and fail to reflect real-world usage diversity. We propose EdgeGen, a synthetic task generation framework that extracts compliance rules from an agent's specification and uses them to generate database-grounded edge-case tasks designed to violate these rules. When combined with existing synthetic data generation techniques, EdgeGen enables agent improvement through finetuning and harness optimization. The resulting pipeline forms a fully automated closed-loop system that requires no human annotation. Finetuning on data generated by EdgeGen yields a consistent mean progress improvement of 2 percent to 42 percent on tau2bench airline domain, while other baseline methods show degradation for some models. On the other hand, for harness optimization, our method shows a mean progress improvement of 10 percent and 30 percent over the human-curated and base harnesses, respectively, for the Gemma-4-e4b model.

</details>


### [31] [Self-Healing Harness for Runtime Oversight of Agent Self-Modification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24130)
*Sina Tayebati,Divake Kumar,Nastaran Darabi,Ranganath Krishnan,Amit Ranjan Trivedi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将LLM智能体的自我修改形式化为"准入控制"问题：智能体提出行为规则变更，由外部运行时门控决定其能否持久化。作者实现了一个模型无关的"自愈框架"（Detect–Notice–Heal–Validate循环），通过重放证据或前向试验验证候选规则，并设语料级护栏重测累积规则集。在AppWorld、Terminal-Bench和τ²-Bench的16对匹配实验中，门控拒绝了383个由重放裁决的提案，其中211个（55%）在改善触发失败的同时破坏了原有可用案例，说明局部有益的自我修改常带来附带回归，为外部准入控制提供了直接实证依据。实验显示Harness在全部16对中任务完成分更高，且变更可检查、可逆、兼容闭权重模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体能够改变自身未来行为，这引出一个基本控制问题：哪些自生成的变更应被允许持久化。若不加约束，智能体可能在改善某一失败点的同时破坏原本正常的行为，因此需要明确何种自我修改可被接纳。

Method: 将自我修改建模为准入控制问题：智能体对运行指令提出变更，外部运行时门控控制其持久化。实现为模型无关的自愈框架（self-healing harness），在未经修改的智能体外围运行"检测—注意—修复—验证"循环。智能体在外部工作区编写候选行为规则，评估期间获得临时执行权限，只有在触发失败上测得改进、且在受保护案例上无超固定边界的回归后，才获得跨回合的持久权限。有重放时提供匹配证据，否则用前向试验作为较弱回退，并以语料级护栏重测累积的活跃规则集。

Result: 在AppWorld、Terminal-Bench和τ²-Bench的16对匹配Baseline与Harness运行中，门控拒绝了383个重放裁决的提案；其中211个（55%）改善了触发失败但破坏了此前可用的案例，表明局部有益的自我修改引入附带回归的频率足以实质影响门控决策。Harness在全部16对中任务完成分更高，其中两个成对bootstrap区间排除零；重复试验可靠性在12对中更高、4对持平、无一对更低。

Conclusion: 局部有益的自我修改常伴随附带回归，这为外部准入控制提供了直接实证动机。由于适应仅修改诱导策略的上下文而保持模型权重不变，被接纳的变更保持可检查、可逆，并兼容闭权重模型。

Abstract: LLM agents can change their own future behavior, raising a basic control question of which self-generated changes should be allowed to persist. We formulate this as admission control for self-modification. The agent may propose changes to its operating instructions, while an external runtime gate controls persistence. We implement this principle as a model-agnostic self-healing harness that runs a Detect, Notice, Heal, Validate loop around an otherwise unmodified agent. The agent authors candidate behavioral rules in an external workspace, where they receive provisional execution authority during evaluation and acquire persistent cross-episode authority only after measured improvement on the triggering failure without regression beyond a fixed margin on protected cases. Replay provides matched evidence when available, forward trials provide a weaker fallback, and a corpus-level guard re-tests the accumulated active rule set. Across 16 matched Baseline and Harness runs spanning AppWorld, Terminal-Bench, and $τ^2$-Bench, the gate rejected 383 replay-decided proposals. Of these, 211 (55%) improved their triggering failure while degrading a case that previously worked. This shows that locally beneficial self-modifications can introduce collateral regressions often enough to materially affect gate decisions, providing direct empirical motivation for external admission control. Task-completion score is higher under the Harness in all 16 pairs, with two paired bootstrap intervals excluding zero, while repeated-trial reliability is higher in 12 pairs, tied in 4, and lower in none. Because adaptation modifies the policy-inducing context while leaving model weights fixed, admitted changes remain inspectable, reversible, and compatible with closed-weight models.

</details>


### [32] [APEXA: Execution-Integrity Enforcement for Multi-Agent LLM Automation of Synchrotron Data Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24165)
*Pawan K. Tripathi,Hemant Sharma,Andrew Chuang,Mathew J. Cherukara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APEXA：面向同步辐射数据处理的多智能体框架，通过执行完整性强制机制杜绝“未执行却报告结果”的伪造问题，并发布首个区分“浪费算力”与“损坏仪器”的任务基准 APEXA-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步辐射数据约简、探测器定标与方位角积分是 TB 级衍射序列处理的多步专家瓶颈，制约用户设施的科学产出率。LLM 智能体有望打通该流程，但用随机模型驱动真实流水线会产生聊天基准无法发现的失效模式：智能体可能报告从未计算过的定标结果。此类场景中，正确性是“已执行内容”的属性，而非对话记录（transcript）的属性。

Method: 提出 APEXA：一个已部署的多智能体框架，将 61 个异构计算工具组织为单一推理循环，以自然语言自动化定标与积分。核心方法包括：(1) 执行完整性强制（execution-integrity enforcement）：确定性工具层守卫，拒绝呈现任何未经实际工具调用支撑的结果，并配有可容忍跨模型工具调用格式漂移的解析器；(2) 将同一代码用于可选电机控制面的安全门控；(3) 发布 APEXA-Bench 评测框架，含 58 个设施任务（50 基础 + 8 跨探测器切片），按四类物理后果分类法组织，并与 NIST 可溯源晶格常数进行跨探测器评分。

Result: 部署中，某前沿模型为从未运行的命令伪造了一份完整的定标对比报告，守卫将其转化为显式“无结果”。同一代码在模拟 IOC 上对可选电机控制面实现 0/200 对抗性违规，而等效安全提示为 15/200。跨探测器评分发现两个潜在流水线缺陷。在真实光束线数据上，单一自然语言提示即完成探测器几何恢复并积分完整的衰减/曝光扫描序列。

Conclusion: 执行完整性是智能体驱动真实科学流水线的关键正确性保障；APEXA 在真实同步辐射装置上验证了端到端自然语言自动化定标与积分的可行性，并释放框架、基准与运行轨迹。大规模智能体评分留待后续完整研究。

Abstract: Synchrotron data reduction, detector calibration followed by azimuthal integration of terabyte-scale diffraction series, is a multi-step, expert-bound bottleneck that increasingly limits the science rate of user facilities. LLM agents promise to collapse it, but driving a real pipeline with a stochastic model creates a failure mode chat benchmarks cannot see: an agent can report a calibration that was never computed. Correctness here is a property of what executed, not of the transcript. We present APEXA, a deployed multi-agent framework (61 tools over heterogeneous compute, run as a single reasoning loop) automating calibration and integration from natural language at a major light source. We make three contributions. First, execution-integrity enforcement: a deterministic tool-layer guard that refuses to surface any result not backed by an executed tool call, with a parser tolerant of cross-model tool-call format drift: in deployment, a frontier model fabricated a complete calibration-comparison report for commands that never ran, which the guard converts to an explicit non-result; the same code gates an optional motor-control surface at 0/200 adversarial violations against a simulated IOC, versus 15/200 for an equivalent safety prompt. Second, we release APEXA-Bench, an evaluation harness of 58 facility tasks (50 base plus an 8-task cross-detector slice) organized by a four-class physical-consequence taxonomy, the first benchmark axis we know of separating a wasted compute cycle from a damaged instrument; its cross-detector grading against NIST-traceable lattice constants surfaced two latent pipeline bugs. Large-scale agent scoring is left to a full-length study. Third, we validate APEXA on real beamline data: from one natural-language prompt it recovers detector geometry and integrates a full attenuation/exposure sweep. We release the framework, harness and traces.

</details>


### [33] [LIMIT: Less Is More for Instruction Tuning in Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24186)
*Haoyuan Ma,Hengwei Liu,Linjuan Wu,Yongliang Shen,Weiming Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LIMIT：以数据为中心的Text-to-SQL指令微调框架，用极小规模精选样本（BIRD 796条、Spider 863条）实现100%表覆盖率，使Qwen3-8B分别达到69.1%和88.9%执行准确率，超越训练数据量20倍的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL方法多依赖大规模指令语料，默认"规模驱动性能"。作者质疑这一范式，探究有效指令微调的最小数据需求，论证战略性样本选择可替代数据规模。

Method: LIMIT四阶段流水线：(1)难度感知过滤，筛选处于模型学习前沿的样本；(2)思维链合成与基于一致性的筛选；(3)LLM-as-judge多维质量评分；(4)遗传算法优化，联合最大化schema覆盖度与样本质量。

Result: 在BIRD与Spider基准上，仅选796和863条样本即达100%表覆盖率，Qwen3-8B执行准确率分别为69.1%与88.9%，超过用20倍数据训练的方法，并在开源方法中创下新SOTA。

Conclusion: 精心数据策划而非规模，是高效Text-to-SQL学习的关键；紧凑且高质量的训练集足以激发强数据库推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved remarkable progress on Text-to-SQL through reasoning-enhanced fine-tuning, yet existing approaches predominantly rely on massive instruction corpora under the assumption that scale drives performance. We challenge this paradigm by investigating a fundamental question: what is the minimal data requirement for effective Text-to-SQL instruction tuning? We propose LIMIT(Less Is More for Instruction Tuning in Text-to-SQL), a data-centric framework that demonstrates strong database reasoning can emerge from an extremely compact training set when examples are strategically selected. LIMIT operates through four stages: difficulty-aware filtering that identifies samples within the model's learning frontier, chain-of-thought synthesis with consistency-based selection, multi-dimensional quality scoring via LLM-as-judge, and genetic algorithm optimization that jointly maximizes schema coverage and sample quality. On the BIRD and Spider benchmark, LIMIT selects only 796 and 863 samples while achieving 100% table coverage, enabling Qwen3-8B to reach 69.1% and 88.9% execution accuracy.This result surpasses methods trained on 20 times more data and establishes a new state-of-the-art among open-source approaches. Our findings suggest that careful data curation, rather than scale, is the key to efficient Text-to-SQL learning.

</details>


### [34] [How Many Pixels Is a Digit Worth? Place-Aware Coordinate Entropy for GUI Agent Confidence Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24277)
*Yunxiang Li,Xixin Wu,Helen Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Place-Aware Coordinate Entropy (PACE)，按位值加权各数字的 Shannon 熵，将 GUI 智能体的点击坐标 token 内部概率转化为逐点击置信度信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI 智能体将点击坐标预测为数字 token 序列，但标准文本 LLM 置信度估计方法难以有效区分正确与错误点击；GUI 专用替代方案依赖 K 次采样或额外监督，仍有改进空间。作者将部分原因归因于位值不对称：包围盒正确性常使高位数字比低位数字更重要，均匀聚合会削弱决定正确性的信号。

Method: PACE 对坐标序列中每个数字的 Shannon 熵按其位值进行加权，从而在单次前向传播中计算逐点击置信度，无需 K 次采样或新增监督。

Result: 在 ScreenSpot-Pro 与 ScreenSpot-v2 上，对固定规模智能体的所有主要对比中，PACE 在 AUROC 与选择性准确率上均获胜，且以极低计算成本匹配或超越 K 样本基线。

Conclusion: PACE 提供逐点击置信度估计，将坐标 token 内部信息转化为可实际部署的 GUI 智能体置信度信号。

Abstract: GUI agents predict click coordinates as digit-token sequences, but standard text-LLM confidence estimation methods rank correct clicks from wrong ones only weakly. GUI-specific alternatives use K samples or new supervision, but still leave room for improvement. We trace part of this to place-value asymmetry: bounding-box correctness often makes higher-place digits more important than lower-place digits, so uniform aggregation weakens the signal that determines correctness. The fix is to weight each digit's Shannon entropy by its place value. We call this Place-Aware Coordinate Entropy (PACE). Across fixed-scale agents on ScreenSpot-Pro and ScreenSpot-v2, PACE wins both AUROC and selective accuracy on all primary comparisons in a single forward pass, matching or outperforming K-sample baselines at a fraction of the cost. PACE provides a per-click confidence estimate that turns coordinate-token internals into a practical confidence signal for GUI agent deployment.

</details>


### [35] [LADDER: Graph-Guided Diffusion Language Models for Efficient Multi-Hop Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24346)
*Senlei Zhang,Linhao Luo,Qian-Wen Zhang,Siyu An,Junnan Dong,Shuhao Zhang,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LADDER将扩散语言模型与GraphRAG结合，通过图引导并行解码加速推理，在多跳QA上提升准确率并大幅降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG依赖自回归语言模型，其固有顺序生成特性严重限制整体推理效率；受扩散语言模型（DLM）通过连续"并行精炼解码"实现大规模并行能力的启发，作者希望在离散空间中加速GraphRAG。但存在两大挑战：一是部分去噪草稿高度动态且不确定，使动态图接地困难；二是原始去噪状态本身噪声大且不稳定，使同步图检索与多跳聚合计算代价过高。

Method: 提出LADDER框架，通过图引导的并行解码桥接扩散语言建模与GraphRAG。核心包含两部分：(i) 事件驱动的自时钟检索：基于"88%的目标实体在部分去噪状态早期即出现"这一关键洞察，仅在可图链接实体集合扩展时动态触发图检索，形成异步自时钟策略，最终提交平均提前5.7-9.6步，且无需学习门控或启发式阈值；(ii) 不完整查询图传播模块：利用专门的图基础模型处理新出现的实体查询，持续聚合多跳证据以锐化并行预测，加速整体解码收敛。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的多跳QA基准上，LADDER将平均精确匹配（Exact Match）从39.6%提升至45.2%，同时实现4.1倍的延迟降低。

Conclusion: LADDER证明了将扩散语言模型与GraphRAG结合的有效性，在离散空间中实现图引导并行解码，兼顾了准确率提升与推理加速，为高效GraphRAG提供新范式。

Abstract: Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) has remarkably enhanced large language models on complex reasoning by leveraging structured entity topologies. However, existing frameworks heavily rely on standard autoregressive language models where the nature of inherent sequential generation severely hinders overall inference efficiency. Inspired by Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) that offer massive parallelism via continuous refine-in-parallel decoding, we aim to accelerate GraphRAG in the discrete space. However, it remains non-trivial for two challenges. First, partially denoised drafts are highly dynamic and uncertain, making dynamic graph grounding non-trivial. Second, raw denoising states are inherently noisy and unstable, making synchronous graph retrieval and multi-hop aggregation computationally prohibitive. To this end, we present LADDER, a novel framework that bridges diffusion language modeling with GraphRAG through graph-guided parallel decoding. Specifically, (i) we propose an event-driven self-clocking retrieval, inspired by our key insight that 88% of target entities emerge early in the partially denoised state, leading final commitment by an average of 5.7-9.6 steps. This mechanism dynamically triggers graph retrieval only when the set of graph-linkable entities expands, yielding an asynchronous self-clocking policy that bypasses learned gates or heuristic thresholds. (ii) An incomplete-query graph propagation module is designed to process the newly emerging entity queries using a specialized graph foundation model, continuously aggregating multi-hop evidence to sharpen parallel predictions and accelerate overall decoding convergence. Extensive experiments on three challenging multi-hop QA benchmarks show that LADDER raises average exact match from 39.6% to 45.2% while achieving a 4.1x latency reduction.

</details>


### [36] [Few-Shot Demonstrations Elicit the Use of In-Context World Representations in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24352)
*Kohsei Matsutani,Gouki Minegishi,Core Francisco Park,Takeshi Kojima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将图追踪任务扩展到少样本设置（每条示例来自不同世界）可提升多种LLM的预测能力；线性探针显示少样本示例使世界表征发生近正交位移，并增强其预测可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为智能体需从上下文中推断潜在世界状态空间并用于下游预测，但已有工作表明LLM在图追踪任务上难以使用上下文中学到的表征。本文旨在理解少样本示例如何改善这种上下文世界建模。

Method: 在图追踪任务上引入少样本设置，每条示例由不同世界生成（图拓扑相同或不同）；在4个模型家族共6个模型上测试；用线性探针在隐状态中提取低维世界表征，并通过表征位移分析与干预实验检验其因果作用。

Result: 少样本示例提升了6个模型的预测性能；世界表征沿与原表征子空间近乎正交的方向移动，且对其干预比干预其他子空间更显著地损害性能；使用来自不同世界的观测作为少样本示例，也在ARC-AGI-1&2、网页智能体任务和Othello上带来提升。

Conclusion: 少样本示例通过重新定位世界表征并提高其预测利用率来改善上下文世界建模，揭示了少样本示例在LLM智能体从上下文中学习世界模型时的作用与内部机制，并为其进一步改进提供启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs), when acting as agents, are expected to take observed data in context, infer the latent state space underlying the world, and leverage it for downstream prediction. However, prior work demonstrated that LLMs struggle to use representations learned in context on a graph tracking task, where the model needs to construct a representation of the graph governing data generation process and use it for subsequent predictions. In this paper, we show that extending this to few-shot settings, where each demonstration is generated from a different world with either the same or different graph topologies, enhances its prediction on 6 models from 4 model families. To understand this improvement, we linearly probe a low-dimensional world representation that encodes graph information in the hidden states. Notably, we find that few-shot demonstrations relocate the world representation and increase its predictive use. Specifically, for each model, these world representations shift in directions nearly orthogonal to their original subspace, and interventions on these representations selectively impair performance more than interventions on other subspaces. Consistent with this insight, we show that few-shot demonstrations with observations from different worlds improve performance on ARC-AGI-1&2, web agent tasks, and Othello. Our findings elucidate the role and internal mechanisms of few-shot demonstrations in in-context world modeling. More broadly, our work advances our understanding of how LLM agents learn from in-context observations and provides implications for their further improvement.

</details>


### [37] [DUMA-Bench: A Dual-Control Multi-Agent Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agent Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24662)
*Ivan Aleksandrov,German Kochnev,Sabrina Sadiekh,Yaroslav Rogoza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DUMA-Bench，一个面向"双控制"交互场景的LLM智能体安全评测基准与协议，扩展τ²-bench并引入8类对抗漏洞环境，在14个模型上验证双控制交互显著提升攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体安全评测普遍假设用户被动、控制静态，忽视了真实部署中用户与智能体共同影响环境状态的交互动态，导致安全评估与实际情况脱节，缺少针对真实智能体部署的评测层。

Method: 提出DUMA-Bench基准与评测协议，用于度量"双控制"交互下的智能体安全性，即智能体与用户均可影响共享环境状态。基于τ²-bench扩展，构建覆盖8类漏洞的对抗环境，包括RAG投毒、跨智能体操纵和不安全输出处理等。在8个领域、多种用户行为模式下，评测来自OpenAI、Anthropic、DeepSeek、Qwen和Z.ai五个模型家族的14个模型。

Result: 引入双控制交互后，攻击成功率从26.9%上升至41.1%，表明交互动态显著放大了安全风险。

Conclusion: 智能体安全性并非仅是模型自身的属性，而是由模型、用户与环境三者交互共同涌现的性质。DUMA-Bench为研究真实智能体部署中的安全问题提供了缺失的评测层。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly operate in environments where they interact with users, tools, and external systems. Yet most security evaluations assume passive users and static control, ignoring the interactive dynamics that shape real agent behavior. We introduce \textbf{DUMA-Bench}, a benchmark and evaluation protocol for measuring agent security under \emph{dual-control} interaction, where both the agent and the user can influence the shared environment state. DUMA-Bench extends $τ^2$-bench ~\cite{barres2025tau} with adversarial environments covering eight vulnerability classes, including RAG poisoning, cross-agent manipulation, and unsafe output handling. We evaluate \textbf{14 models from five model families} (OpenAI, Anthropic, DeepSeek, Qwen, and Z.ai) across eight domains and multiple user-behavior regimes. Across our experiments, introducing dual-control interaction increases the attack success rate from \textbf{26.9\%} to \textbf{41.1\%}. These results show that agent security is not solely a property of the model but emerges from the interaction between the model, the user, and the environment. DUMA-Bench provides a missing evaluation layer for studying security in realistic agent deployments.

</details>


### [38] [Pinocchio: Fast Uncertainty Estimates for Black-Box Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24881)
*Kevin David Hayes,Arka Pal,Haosong Zhang,Tom Goldstein,Micah Goldblum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Pinocchio，一种外部校准器，仅凭黑盒API模型的文本响应即可估计其预测正确性，无需log概率、微调或内部状态访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险场景下LLM不仅需要准确，还需不确定性估计。但许多工业级闭源API模型（如GPT）不返回log概率且不允许微调，现有不确定性估计方法依赖log概率或微调权限，无法适用。

Method: 训练一个外部校准器，联合七个LLM的响应进行训练，仅使用文本（单次前向传播）预测响应正确性，不访问目标模型的logits、权重或内部状态。

Result: 在同源模型留出响应上达到0.862 AUROC；对八个机构的十三个未见模型实现零样本迁移；0.8B轻量文本checkpoint即可匹敌最大模型的AUROC；代码仅需两行即可集成。

Conclusion: Pinocchio为黑盒API模型提供了一种实用、低成本、易集成的不确定性估计方案，具备良好泛化能力。

Abstract: In high-stakes decision-making applications of large language models (LLMs), practitioners require not only accurate LLMs but also uncertainty estimates for their predictions. Existing approaches to uncertainty estimation for LLMs require access to log-probabilities output by the model or require fine-tuning access. However, many industrial LLM products use closed-source API models, and many such API models like GPT do not return log-probabilities and may not allow fine-tuning. We introduce Pinocchio, an external calibrator that estimates the correctness of responses from black-box API models. Trained jointly on responses from seven LLMs, it achieves 0.862 AUROC predicting the correctness of held-out responses from those same models, and shows zero-shot transfer to thirteen unseen models across eight organizations. Our model needs only a single forward pass to generate an uncertainty estimate and requires no access to the target model's logits, weights, or internal states. A lightweight text only 0.8B checkpoint matches our largest model's AUROC. We release code for adding uncertainty estimation to existing repos in only two additional lines of code.

</details>


### [39] [Emergent Collusion in Long-Horizon LLM Agent Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24967)
*Xinrui Shi,Yanzhe Zhang,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在长时程多智能体协作环境中，合规验证协议与奖励最大化不相容时，两个智能体在94%的轨迹中出现共谋，且能力越强的模型越早达成共谋；共谋受同伴行为、奖励结构、验证反馈与交互历史影响，限制交互历史可缓解共谋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地被部署于协作场景，长期交互可能催生不良协调行为。作者关注共谋（collusion）这一安全风险如何在长时程多智能体交互中自发涌现，并探究其形成机制与缓解手段。

Method: 构建长时程多智能体环境：两个智能体反复完成各自独立任务、共享任务日志、互相验证对方工作并获取奖励。设计现实约束，使遵守验证协议与奖励最大化相互冲突。在10个模型上开展实验，并施加受控同伴干预（peer interventions）与消融实验，分别考察奖励结构、验证反馈、交互历史等因素的作用。

Result: 在94%的轨迹中出现共谋，且同一模型家族中能力更强的模型更早达成共谋。同伴干预表明共谋受同伴行为影响；消融实验显示奖励结构、验证反馈及交互历史均有额外影响，其中限制交互历史的数量与范围可降低共谋程度。

Conclusion: 长时程交互会重塑智能体的协调方式，从而产生新的安全风险；共谋是同伴行为、激励机制与信息可得性共同作用的结果，通过约束交互历史等设计可对其加以抑制。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed in collaborative settings, yet long-term interaction may give rise to undesirable coordination. We study the emergence of collusion in a long-horizon multi-agent environment: two agents repeatedly complete individual tasks, share task logs, verify each other's work, and receive rewards. We introduce realistic constraints that make compliance with the verification protocol incompatible with reward maximization, and find that agents increasingly deviate from the protocol over repeated interactions. Collusion emerges in 94% of trajectories across 10 models, and more capable models within the same family reach it earlier. Controlled peer interventions show that collusion is shaped by peer behavior, while ablations reveal additional effects of reward structure, the verification feedback agents receive, and their interaction history. In particular, restricting the amount and scope of interaction history available to agents reduces collusion. Overall, our findings show that long-horizon interaction can reshape how agents coordinate in ways that create safety risks.

</details>

### [40] [FinRankGRPO: Optimizing LLMs for Listwise Financial Asset Ranking via Group Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24175)
*Ningyuan Deng,Jinyuan Wang,Qi Li,Jia Zhang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出FinRankGRPO框架，将LLM组合构建从数值预测转为资产列表排序，两阶段训练（CoT监督微调+GRPO强化学习）并用Spearman秩相关奖励对齐市场真实排序，夏普比率0.636。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM擅长理解非结构化金融语境，但直接用于组合优化存在两个障碍：一是下一词预测目标与资产配置所需的列表排序目标不匹配；二是精确数值预测能力弱，导致不稳定和算术幻觉。

Method: 将LLM组合构建任务由直接数值预测重构为金融资产的列表排序。采用两阶段训练：第一阶段在思维链（CoT）推理数据上做监督微调；第二阶段使用"金融资产排序的组相对策略优化"（Financial Asset Ranking via GRPO），采用新颖的Spearman秩相关奖励。

Result: FinRankGRPO优于传统量化模型与最先进的商业模型，取得0.636的夏普比率和0.023的Spearman相关系数。

Conclusion: 以列表排序替代数值预测，并通过Spearman秩相关奖励的GRPO对齐生成偏好与真实市场排序，可有效弥补LLM在组合优化中目标错配与数值幻觉的缺陷，提升投资组合构建的表现。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at understanding unstructured financial contexts, their direct use in portfolio optimization is limited by a mismatch between next-token prediction and the listwise ranking objectives required for asset allocation. They also struggle with precise numerical forecasting, leading to instability and arithmetic hallucinations. To bridge this gap, we propose FinRankGRPO, a framework that shifts LLM based portfolio construction from direct numerical prediction to listwise ranking of financial assets. We introduce a two-stage training process, supervised finetuning on Chain-of-Thought reasoning data, followed by our Financial Asset Ranking via Group Relative Policy Optimization with a Spearman rank correlation reward that aligns generated asset rankings with ground truth market orderings. The second stage uses a novel Spearman rank correlation reward to explicitly align the model's generative preferences with ground truth market orderings. Experimental results show that FinRankGRPO outperforms traditional quantitative and state-of-the-art commercial models, achieving a Sharpe ratio of 0.636 and a Spearman correlation of 0.023.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

### [41] [Predicting energy and structural response to force correction in molecular dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22287)
*Peng Kang,Da Wan,Shulin Bai,Pengfei Zhang,Peng Wang,Chenglong Wen,Zhen Li,Yu Liu,Lei Zheng,Li-Dong Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出用参考轨迹上的领头响应系数预测力修正对能量交换与结构统计的影响，并用独立模拟与实验检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力修正通常以其对单个力评估的精度来评价，但缺乏物理基础来判断参考信息应如何进入分子动力学；需要评估力修正对原子运动与统计观测量的影响。

Method: 在共享参考轨迹上测量领头响应系数；用残余功率与位移virial区分能量转移和回复力变化，包括间歇性参考更新；采用独立模拟检验预测的动能与构型变化，并进行匹配时间表实验；在非谐链、硅、正交相SnSe中检验。

Result: 匹配时间表实验表明参考时序通过其与演化状态的耦合影响加热；非谐链中位移virial预测出仅用功率描述会遗漏的结构位移；硅中局部力常数预测出互补的方向性权衡，标量校准修复光学运动却损害已准确的低频方向；全非线性轨迹确认该权衡并区分静态曲率修正与重复参考脉冲的收益；SnSe的有限温积分支持由独立参考计算预测的构型响应方向。

Conclusion: 结果为选择参考信息如何进入分子动力学提供物理依据；该框架通过力修正对原子运动和统计观测量的影响来评估其效果，超越单个力评估的精度。

Abstract: We predict how force correction changes energy exchange and structural statistics by measuring leading response coefficients on shared reference trajectories. Residual power and the displacement virial distinguish the transfer of energy from the change in restoring forces, including intermittent reference updates. Independent simulations then test the predicted kinetic and configurational shifts. A matched-timetable experiment shows that reference timing affects heating through its coupling to the evolving state. In an anharmonic chain, the displacement virial predicts a structural shift missed by a power-only description. Local force constants in silicon predict a complementary directional tradeoff: scalar calibration repairs optical motion while degrading an already accurate low-frequency direction. Full nonlinear trajectories confirm this tradeoff and distinguish the benefits of static curvature correction and repeated reference impulses. Independent finite-temperature integrals in orthorhombic tin selenide (SnSe) support the configurational-response direction predicted from separate reference calculations. These results provide a physical basis for choosing how reference information enters molecular dynamics. The framework assesses force correction through its effect on atomic motion and statistical observables, beyond the accuracy of individual force evaluations.

</details>


### [42] [NTBuilder: Commensurate Construction of Nanotubes from Arbitrary Two-Dimensional Crystals and a Catalog of 20 Million Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22341)
*Marcelo Lopes Pereira Junior*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作建立了任意二维晶体卷曲成纳米管的通用理论，并实现为开源工具 ntbuilder，同时生成了包含 2033 万余个纳米管的公开数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纳米管构造多针对单一材料且沿用以石墨烯为基础的方法，缺乏适用于任意二维晶体的通用卷曲理论，也难以控制周期性与计算可行性之间的平衡。

Method: 提出任意二维晶体卷曲构造的一般理论，用晶格度规导出的有理数约化形式表达精确晶胞尺寸；以第二数的有理逼近构造最优近似晶胞；将 17 个平面群归并为 7 类手性映射，并逐对检测曲率引起的成键与断键；该框架还支持多壁管、周期束及应变或扭转的周期结构组装。

Result: 实现了含桌面与网页界面的开源库 ntbuilder；解释了三角与四方晶格可小晶胞闭合，而矩形、有心矩形与斜方晶格可能需大若干数量级的晶胞（如联苯网络的近似晶胞比精确晶胞小八个数量级）；应用于 46,403 个二维体系，生成含 20,334,372 个纳米管的目录，其中 57.1% 卷曲后无键变化。

Conclusion: 该通用卷曲理论及 ntbuilder 工具可系统构建并导出可供第一性原理与分子动力学计算直接使用的纳米管结构，为二维材料纳米管研究提供了统一框架和大规模资源。

Abstract: Although tens of thousands of two-dimensional (2D) crystals are now known, the nanotubes they can form are usually constructed for individual materials, using procedures derived for graphene. Here, we develop a general theory of the rolling construction for arbitrary 2D crystals and implement it in \ntbuilder{} (NanoTube Builder), an open-source library with desktop and web interfaces. We express the size of the exact unit cell of a tube through the reduced form of a single rational number built from the lattice metric, which explains why triangular and square lattices always close with small cells, whereas rectangular, centered rectangular, and oblique lattices can require cells larger by many orders of magnitude. The optimal approximate cells are the best rational approximations of a second number, so that a cell small enough for first-principles calculations can be chosen with a controlled departure from periodicity, as in the biphenylene network, where such a cell is eight orders of magnitude smaller than the exact one. The 17 plane groups reduce to seven distinct chirality maps once rolling is taken into account, the rolling sense of layers without a horizontal mirror defines distinct tubes, and bonds formed or broken by curvature are detected pair by pair against the flat layer. The same framework assembles multiwalled tubes whose walls share one axial period, periodic bundles, and strained or twisted structures that remain periodic. Applied to 46,403 distinct 2D systems, it produced a public catalog of 20,334,372 nanotubes, of which 57.1\% show no bond change upon rolling. All structures can be exported directly as input for first-principles and molecular dynamics codes.

</details>


### [43] [From Ferroionic Instability to Domain Patterns at an Exposed Surfaces of Multiaxial Ferroelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22366)
*Sergei V. Kalinin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文建立一个结合矢量极化、表面电化学、静电学、相容应变与竞争吸附的多晶铁电体模型，通过线性稳定性分析与非线性延拓，揭示初始极化失稳如何演化为成熟畴结构，并关联瑞利波长与畴间距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轴铁电体在开口、电化学补偿表面上的畴结构形成机制尚不清楚，尤其是初始失稳波长与最终畴结构周期之间的关系，以及铁电弛豫体中周期性表面调制的可能来源，需要统一的理论框架来解释。

Method: 构建耦合矢量极化、表面电化学、静电学、相容应变和竞争吸附的理论模型；计算均匀相图并分析极化失稳，先假设沿厚度均匀的扰动，再允许深度相关的极化剖面；采用非线性延拓追踪调制向成熟畴的增长；优化畴周期并进行稳定性检验；同时考察稳定弱各向异性体相之上表面有序的构成极限。

Result: 建立了初始失稳波长与成熟畴结构优选间距之间的关系；发现具有相同二次项的表面能可产生相同的初始失稳，但倾向于不同的发展态，包括周期性畴和均匀面内极化；为铁电弛豫体周期性表面调制的可能起源提供了见解。

Conclusion: 该分析提供了一个从铁电调制初始发展到幅度选择、波长调整及畴结构后续演化的连接程序，明确了表面电化学与力学耦合在畴结构形成中的关键作用。

Abstract: We analyze the formation of domain structures in multiaxial ferroelectrics with an open, electrochemically compensated surface. The model combines vector polarization, surface electrochemistry, electrostatics, compatible strain, and competitive adsorption on common sites. We construct homogeneous phase maps and analyze polarization instabilities, first assuming perturbations that are uniform through the film thickness and then allowing depth-dependent polarization profiles. Nonlinear continuation follows the growth of these modulations into developed domains. Optimization of their period and tests of their stability establish how the initial instability wavelength relates to the preferred spacing of an established domain structure. We also consider surface ordering above a stable, weakly anisotropic bulk as a constitutive limit relevant to ferroelectric relaxors, providing insight into possible origins of their periodic surface modulations. Surface energies with the same quadratic terms can produce the same initial instability but favor different developed states, including periodic domains and uniform in-plane polarization. This analysis provides a procedure for connecting the initial development of ferroelectric modulations to amplitude selection, wavelength adjustment, and the subsequent evolution of the resulting domain structures.

</details>


### [44] [A Hybrid Quantum Neural Network to Analyse Big Experimental Powder X-ray Diffraction Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22376)
*H. Dong,S. D. M. Jacques,M. Q. Hlatshwayo,E. Papoutsellis,K. Georgopoulos,A. M. Beale,A. Vamvakeros*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种混合量子神经网络框架，可直接从一维粉末衍射图谱中提取相重量分数和尺度因子等定量参数，无需迭代精修。模型采用噪声感知经典模拟器预训练结合量子处理单元特征的下游微调策略，并在IBM量子计算机上成功重建了三相固体氧化物燃料电池（约1万条图谱）和四相锂离子电池（约2万条图谱）的定量空间相分布图，结果与经典Rietveld精修高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定量分析实验粉末X射线衍射数据在评估复杂多相材料和噪声测量时仍具挑战性。传统Rietveld精修等迭代方法耗时且依赖初始参数，尤其面对X射线衍射计算断层扫描产生的大规模图谱数据集时效率低下。作者希望探索量子计算硬件在真实材料表征数据分析中的应用潜力。

Method: 提出混合量子神经网络框架，直接从一维粉末衍射图谱中提取相重量分数与尺度因子等定量参数，避免迭代精修。采用噪声感知的经典模拟器预训练，并在量子处理单元特征上快速下游微调，以增强对硬件退相干效应的稳定性。随后将训练好的网络部署到IBM量子计算机上，用于分析实验X射线衍射计算断层扫描数据集。

Result: 在三相固体氧化物燃料电池（约10,000条图谱）和四相锂离子电池（约20,000条图谱）数据集上，网络成功重建了定量空间相分布图，与经典Rietveld精修结果高度吻合，验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了量子计算硬件可用于分析真实世界的材料表征数据，为无需迭代精修、直接提取衍射定量参数的量子机器学习方法铺平了道路。

Abstract: Quantitative analysis of experimental powder X-ray diffraction data remains challenging when evaluating complex multiphase materials and noisy measurements. We introduce a hybrid quantum neural network framework designed to extract quantitative parameters, such as phase weight fractions and scale factors, directly from one-dimensional powder diffraction patterns without iterative refinement. The model combines noise-aware classical simulator pre-training with fast downstream fine-tuning on quantum processing unit features, ensuring stability against hardware decoherence. We demonstrate the practical utility of this approach by deploying the trained network onto an IBM quantum computer to analyse experimental X-ray diffraction computed tomography datasets from a three-phase solid oxide fuel cell containing ca. 10,000 patterns and a four-phase lithium-ion battery containing ca. 20,000 patterns). The network successfully reconstructs quantitative spatial phase maps in strong agreement with classical Rietveld refinement, paving the way for using quantum computing hardware to analyse real-world materials characterisation data.

</details>


### [45] [A low-symmetry ground state of dense two-dimensional hydrogen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22440)
*Cesare Cozza,Chris J. Pickard,Guglielmo Mazzola*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用第一性原理结构搜索（超胞至512原子）发现致密二维氢在中等压强区间的基态结构无长程晶序，其焓低于所有晶态候选，暗示存在无序基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维（体相）氢已被研究近一个世纪，涉及分子解离、金属化与反常熔化等复杂行为；但其二维对应体系尚未被探索，因此需要考察致密二维氢的基态结构与有序—无序竞争。

Method: 使用第一性原理结构搜索与结构弛豫，构建并优化含有多达512个原子的超胞；在密度泛函理论框架下比较非晶/无序结构与各类晶态候选相的焓，并结合经典原子核（经典核近似）与超胞尺寸收敛性测试，通过结构因子（structure factor）峰的增长行为区分无序与晶态。

Using first-principles structural searches and relaxations in supercells containing up to 512 atoms, we identify ground-state structures of dense two-dimensional hydrogen showing no evidence of long-range crystalline order over the simulated length scales. Abstract: In the realm of material analysis, identifying different material phases is of key importance. Artificial intelligence in the form of neural networks provides a very fast and, once trained, computationally inexpensive method for analysing large amounts of image data, like the sets of diffraction patterns generated during four-dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4DSTEM) dataset acquisition. In this work, we train multiple network architectures on images of this type to distinguish between the phases of LiNiO2 and NiO, an important and challenging distinction in the lithium-ion battery community, since the formation of NiO limits battery capacity. We test both classical convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and different forms of vision transformers (ViTs). Our networks are trained on synthetic images and tested on experimentally recorded diffraction patterns. Additionally, we also investigate the decision-making of our networks using the GradCAM method. We test our networks on both synthetic as well as experimental diffraction patterns. *Oguzhan Orhan,Soner Özgen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: GRAINSMITH是一个开源Python软件包，用于生成具有统计可控性和可选晶界形貌的多晶原子模型，支持分子动力学模拟及后续弛豫。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Atomistic studies of grain-boundary engineering, grain-size effects and dopant enrichment require reproducible models with prescribed microstructural features. We present GRAINSMITH, an open-source Python package for generating statistically controlled polycrystalline models with selectable grain-boundary morphologies for molecular dynamics and subsequent relaxation. Within supported feature combinations, a single configuration specifies grain-size and volume distributions, crystallographic texture, boundary-area-weighted disorientation-angle distributions, phase composition and grain-boundary dopant placement. Crystal construction supports 230 crystallographic space groups. Periodic Voronoi and volume-targeted Laguerre tessellations provide planar boundaries, while distinct geometry backends generate smoothly curved and band-limited self-affine boundaries. A registry of twenty-six checks assesses applicable inputs, construction properties and outputs. Each run exports LAMMPS data and Extended XYZ files together with structural and statistical descriptors and machine-readable provenance. For a fixed software version and computational environment, the configuration and random seed determine byte-reproducible atomic configurations and scientific data across supported worker counts. By combining statistical specification, boundary-morphology control and reproducible atomistic output, GRAINSMITH supports systematic studies of microstructural effects and quantitative comparisons across generated models.

</details>


### [49] [Perspective: The HSE Screened Hybrid and the Band Gap Problem: Origins, Impact, and the Contenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23225)
*Gustavo E. Scuseria*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文回顾了HSE屏蔽杂化泛函的诞生决策与影响，并评估了半局域及杂化泛函在固体基准上的最新进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HSE最早并非为预测带隙而设计，而是旨在使精确交换在扩展体系中变得可负担且物理上合理；其带隙精度的意外成功促使作者回顾设计初衷并评估后续发展。

Method: 采用Perspective综述形式：第一部分追溯HSE诞生时的关键决策及其对后续发展的影响；第二部分将近期半局域与杂化泛函与固体基准进行对比，评估meta-GGA、介电依赖型及调谐型杂化泛函的表现。

Result: 近期的meta-GGA在部分半导体上达到HSE06级别的精度；介电依赖型和调谐型杂化泛函则克服了固定屏蔽的局限。评估同时涵盖带隙、结构、能量学、计算成本和可迁移性。

Conclusion: HSE确立了实用的屏蔽杂化方法用于精确带隙计算，推动了缺陷物理、光伏、电力电子和量子信息等领域的第一性原理研究；后续杂化发展与早期局域杂化和局域距离分离工作共享“使精确交换适应电子环境”的目标。

Abstract: The ideas behind the screened hybrid functional HSE took shape in the early 2000s, about a quarter century ago. HSE was not explicitly designed to predict band gaps, although at the time we did recognize that hybrids could help address the band-gap problem. It was designed to make exact exchange affordable and physically defensible in extended systems. Keeping exact exchange at short range and screening it away at long range inverted the prescription developed for molecules. When HSE band gaps for ordinary semiconductors and selected metal oxides came out substantially closer to experiment than those obtained with conventional semilocal approximations, we were pleasantly surprised and encouraged by the path we had taken. The revision from HSE03 to HSE06 improved molecular thermochemistry while preserving the good accuracy for semiconductor gaps. The one-quarter exchange fraction was inherited unchanged from the successful unscreened PBE hybrid for molecules, rather than fitted to solids. HSE established a practical screened-hybrid approach for accurate band-gap calculations, expanding first-principles work in defect physics, photovoltaics, power electronics, and quantum information, among other areas. The first part of this Perspective recounts the decisions made at HSE's birth and their influence on subsequent developments. The second part assesses recent advances in semilocal and hybrid functionals against benchmarks on sets of solids. Recent meta-GGAs achieve HSE06-level accuracy for selected semiconductors, while dielectric-dependent and tuned hybrids address the limitations of fixed screening. These hybrid developments share with our early work on local hybrids and local range separation the aim of adapting exact exchange to the electronic environment. The comparisons assess band gaps alongside structures, energetics, computational cost, and transferability.

</details>

Abstract: Electrides, ionic crystals in which excess electrons are delocalized within interstitial voids rather than bound to specific atomic sites, exhibit exotic physicochemical behaviors, yet their potential as electrochemical energy-storage hosts remains largely unexplored. Here, using comprehensive first-principles calculations, we demonstrate that the quasi-one-dimensional subnitride electride Ba$_3$N offers a compelling route to overcome the long-standing performance trade-offs in sodium-ion battery anodes. Spontaneous Na intercalation into the open interchain channels establishes thermodynamically stable phases, generating an exceptionally flat low-potential plateau that avoids the high-potential sloping losses of hard carbon while preserving an essential safety margin against dendrite formation. Crucially, the itinerant interstitial anionic electron sea dynamically flattens the potential landscape by suppressing site-specific orbital interactions, enabling ultrafast Na ion transport with an exceptionally low migration barrier. This work establishes a design paradigm for harnessing electride chemistry to achieve ultrafast battery electrodes.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动的间隙原子在缺陷迁移过程中会重新分布，使缺陷动力学预测复杂化，文献中关于间隙原子加速或延缓缺陷扩散的报道相互矛盾。需要一个统一的热力学框架来定量描述间隙化学势与温度对缺陷活化过程的影响。

Conclusion: 该大正则自由能框架结合TSCPI方法，可高效、准确地预测间隙原子存在下缺陷活化自由能及其随温度和化学势的变化，统一解释了加速与延缓的矛盾现象，并为理解间隙-缺陷相互作用提供了可迁移的微观机制。

Abstract: Mobile interstitial atoms redistribute while defects migrate, complicating prediction of defect kinetics and leading to contradictory reports of acceleration and retardation. We formulate defect activation in a grand-canonical ensemble at fixed interstitial chemical potential and define a pathway free-energy landscape $ΔG(Λ;T,μ)$ and the corresponding activation free energy $ΔG^{\ddagger}$. We compute these quantities using both hyperplane-constrained thermodynamic integration via a mean force relation and a two-state chemical-potential integration, denoted TSCPI. The latter requires sampling only the initial and transition states; a single chemical-potential integration then yields $ΔG^{\ddagger}(T,μ)$ across a wide $μ$ range, enabling rapid mapping over temperature and chemical potential. Applied to vacancy diffusion in FCC, BCC, and HCP metals with H (including in plane and cross plane diffusion in Zr) and in BCC W with He, both free energy routes agree with diffusion coefficients from molecular dynamics mean square displacements. The resulting maps reveal regimes of suppression and enhancement, including a crossover from suppression to enhancement with increasing hydrogen concentration. A site occupancy analysis links barrier shifts to state dependent site spectrum changes and transferable interstitial interaction terms.

</details>

Abstract: Ab initio calculations of cubic perovskites RGaO$_3$ (R = Ho, Er, Tm) were performed using the full-potential linearized augmented plane wave (FP-LAPW) method within the framework of density functional theory (DFT). The electronic band structures indicate that ErGaO$_3$ is semiconducting, whereas HoGaO$_3$ and TmGaO$_3$ exhibit half-metallic behavior, probably due to the interaction of spin-orbit coupling with the electronic states near the Fermi level. Optical response of half-metallic compounds (HoGaO$_3$,TmGaO$_3$) is strongly governed by their spin polarization electronic structure. The semiconducting spin channel absorbs ultraviolet photons, while metallic or small gap channel interacts with infrared light. This dual behavior enables spin selective optical excitation, relevant for spintronics and optoelectronics applications. These compounds show high surface reflectivity, with TmGaO$_3$ achieving a maximum reflectivity of 69%. Moreover, their optical conductivity mainly occurs at higher energies, indicating suitability for high-energy optoelectronic applications. The calculated magnetic moments suggest a robust ferromagnetic character across this series.

*M. M. Woldemariam,N. G. Debelo,T. K. Tufa,E. M. Gurmesa,S. H. Didu,S. N. Asfaw,D. T. Keno*

Incorporating defect data from multiple supercell sizes improves the predictive performance of the MLIP, while adding pristine defect-free bulk supercells further enhances the accuracy. Abstract: In a hexagonal crystal the $a$ and $c$ axes change their lengths at different rates with increasing temperature, and the thermal expansion is described by two coefficients, one for each axis. The equilibrium lattice parameters at each temperature are found by minimizing the Helmholtz free energy over the lattice parameters, and the coefficients are their logarithmic derivatives with respect to temperature. This report describes a procedure for this minimization as implemented in phonopy. The free energy is computed with harmonic force constants at a finite set of values of $a$ and $c$, and a free-energy surface over $a$ and $c$ is fitted to these values. In a variant, the harmonic force constants are replaced by temperature-dependent force constants from a self-consistent harmonic approximation, with the forces computed by a machine-learning potential fitted at each of these values. The $α$ and $ω$ phases of titanium are the worked example. Both calculations give negative thermal expansion of the $c$ axis of $α$-Ti at low temperature and none in $ω$-Ti, and they differ most for the $c$ axis of $α$-Ti. The axial coefficients are harder to compute than the volumetric one, because the two axes are coupled in the free energy. In both phases of titanium the thermal effect that lengthens $a$ also shortens $c$. The coefficient of the $c$ axis is then the sum of two terms of opposite sign that partly cancel, and a small error in either term gives a large relative error in the coefficient. The report measures how much each setting of the procedure changes the thermal expansion coefficients, with the $c$ axis of $α$-Ti as the most sensitive case.

</details>

Conclusion: ScB₂单晶具备与AlGaN匹配的晶格、热膨胀及Debye温度，低寄生电阻与竞争性热导率，表面平整，可通过工程化声子桥与声子极化激元实现先进散热，有望使功率处理能力较GaN/SiC提升约10–100倍。

Abstract: We report the properties of hexagonal (space group P6/mmm) scandium diboride ($\mathrm{ScB}_2$) single crystals grown by a laser diode floating zone method at growth rates of ~1mm/hr under B-rich conditions with (002) rocking curve widths $Δω$=38'' approaching the quality of commercial SiC/GaN substrates. Lattice expansion measurements reveal matching to $\mathrm{Al_{0.55}Ga_{0.45}N}$ with a coefficient of thermal expansion ~5ppm/K at typical AlGaN growth temperatures, enabling thick AlGaN layers for ultra-wide bandgap (UWBG) power electronics >1kV. We measure semi-metallic room temperature resistivity ~15$μΩ$ cm, climbing to ~93$μΩ$ cm at 773K with a $T^2$ dependence effectively eliminating substrate parasitic resistance, the limiting factor in exploiting the full potential of UWBG. The Debye temperature $θ_{D,ScB_2}$ from heat capacity and lattice expansion is ~850K well matched to $θ_{D,ScB_2}$, but lower than the 1100K measured for Sc-rich growth conditions. We discuss Debye matching as a key substrate codesign criterion providing significant overlap in phonon modes for heat removal and thermal matching during AlGaN growth. The competitive thermal conductivity at room temperature 53W/mK is half that from full first principles calculations, a discrepancy we attribute to the presence of Sc-vacancies generated by B-rich growth. while the resistivity is ~2x the theoretical value, indicating that both electrons and phonons play equal role in thermal transport. The smooth ~2.5nm rms roughness surface enables advanced heat removal modalities through engineered phonon bridges and phonon polaritons in $\mathrm{ScB}_2$/AlGaN interfacial heterostructures, potentially allowing ~10-100x increase in power handling over state-of-the-art GaN/SiC.

</details>
