### [61] [XC100: A Wavefunction-Derived Exchange-Correlation Energy Dataset for Atomic and Molecular Species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22490)
*Vaibhav Khanna,Paul M. Zimmerman*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一套从波函数出发构造精确Kohn-Sham交换-关联能的工作流程，构建了含100个闭壳层原子与分子的XC100数据集，并用于评估传统与机器学习密度泛函。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度泛函理论中交换-关联泛函的评估与开发需要高质量的参考数据。传统上缺乏由波函数严格导出的Kohn-Sham交换-关联能参考值，因此需要一条可靠的途径来生成此类基准数据。

Method: 采用极化三重zeta基组cc-pVTZ下的组态相互作用波函数获得相关总能与密度，将每个波函数密度映射到Kohn-Sham行列式。通过复合校正引入价层与芯层基函数（cc-pVQZ与cc-pCVTZ）以及两点Riemann外推以逼近完全基组极限。将该流程应用于100个主族元素构成的闭壳层原子与分子物种，形成XC100数据集。

Result: 所得Kohn-Sham密度能很好地重现相关密度，L2/Ne差异中位数为1.42×10⁻⁵；复合校正相较cc-pVTZ参考恢复了大量相关能。与传统及机器学习密度泛函近似方法的比较，揭示了对不同类型交换-关联模型Exc质量的洞察。

Conclusion: 该工作流程为生成用于密度泛函评估与开发的交换-关联参考数据提供了一条实用途径。

Abstract: A workflow is introduced for constructing accurate, wavefunction-derived Kohn-Sham (KS) exchange-correlation (XC) energies across atomic and molecular species. Correlated total energies and densities are obtained from configuration interaction wave functions in a polarized triple-zeta basis, cc-pVTZ, and each wavefunction density is then mapped onto a KS determinant. A composite correction adds valence and core basis functions (cc-pVQZ and cc-pCVTZ), plus a two-point Riemann extrapolation to approach the complete-basis-set limit. The workflow is applied to 100 closed-shell atomic and molecular species composed of main group elements to form the XC100 data set. The resulting KS densities closely reproduce the correlated densities, with a median L2/Ne difference of 1.42 X 10^{-5}, while the composite corrections recover substantial correlation energy beyond the cc-pVTZ reference. Comparison with conventional and machine-learned density functional approximations gives insight into the quality of Exc across different types of models for XC. In all, this paper's workflow demonstrates a practical route for generating XC reference data for the assessment and development of density functionals.

</details>


### [62] [SPIBER: Reconstructing Free Energy Landscapes from Short, Unconverged Trajectories with Generative Flow Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22663)
*Venkata Sai Sreyas Adury,Pratyush Tiwary*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: SPIBER将状态预测信息瓶颈(SPIB)与生成流网络(GFlowNets)结合，通过学习慢自由度并利用条件平均势能近似自由能差，从而在少量短轨迹、未收敛采样条件下恢复亚稳态间的自由能差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子体系自由度高，但其亚稳态行为常可由少数集体变量描述。识别这些变量并沿其估计自由能仍是从有限模拟数据中亟待解决的关键问题。多条短轨迹可能采样到不同亚稳态，却未捕获态间跃迁，也无法确定其相对平衡布居；对于同一哈密顿量、单一温度下生成的无偏轨迹，基于直方图重加权的方法无法校正这种采样不均衡。

Method: 提出SPIBER，融合状态预测信息瓶颈(SPIB)与生成流网络(GFlowNets)。SPIB通过深度学习，以过去-未来信息瓶颈逼近慢自由度，保留预测未来亚稳态所需信息。该压缩限制了已采样区域中条件熵的波动，使得更易计算的条件平均势能可用于近似自由能差。在具备足够局部采样以估计这些能量的前提下，用其定义GFlowNets的目标分布；GFlowNets作为基于能量的生成采样器，按估计的热力学稳定性而非观测布居进行采样。

Result: 在径向双势阱中的粒子、丙氨酸二肽以及九残基肽AIB9三个体系上，SPIBER恢复的采样亚稳态间自由能差与参考值的偏差在一个热能单位以内。

Conclusion: 该方法在不超过四维潜空间中同时实现集体变量学习与自由能估计，无需收敛的态布居，也无需额外的分子动力学模拟。

Abstract: Molecular systems have many degrees of freedom, but their metastable behavior can often be described by a few collective variables. Identifying these variables and estimating free energies along them from limited simulation data remains a challenging, important problem. Separate short trajectories may sample different metastable states without capturing transitions or establishing their relative equilibrium populations. For unbiased trajectories generated with the same Hamiltonian at a single temperature, alternate methods based on histogram reweighting cannot correct this imbalance. Here we present SPIBER, which combines the State Predictive Information Bottleneck (SPIB) with Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets). SPIB uses deep learning to approximate slow degrees of freedom through a past-future information bottleneck, retaining information needed to predict future metastable states. We show that this compression limits conditional entropy variations in populated regions, allowing conditional mean potential energies, which are much easier to calculate, to be used to approximate free energy differences. Given sufficient local sampling to estimate these energies, they define the target distribution for GFlowNets, energy-based generative samplers that sample according to estimated thermodynamic stability rather than observed populations. For a particle in a radial double-well potential, for alanine dipeptide, and for the nine-residue peptide AIB9, SPIBER recovers free energy differences between sampled metastable states to within one thermal energy unit of reference values. The method combines collective-variable learning and free energy estimation in up to four latent dimensions, without requiring converged state populations or additional molecular dynamics simulations.

</details>


### [63] [ECENet: An Edge Cluster Expansion Line-Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23134)
*R. Allen LaCour,Teresa Head-Gordon*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出边团簇展开（ECE）及基于其构建的线图神经网络 ECENet，采用 O(2) 等变边特征，在精度-成本权衡上达到帕累托前沿，并可预测偶极矩与红外光谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）多采用 O(3) 等变图神经网络，精度高但计算开销大；需要一种既保持物理表达能力又更高效的等变表示框架。

Method: 提出边团簇展开（ECE），将环境围绕原子对之间的边而非单个原子展开，类比原子团簇展开；基于此构建线图神经网络 ECENet，使用持续存在于原子间边上的 O(2) 等变特征，从而利用比 O(3) 更廉价、限制更少的 O(2) 操作；并通过潜在 Ewald 求和引入长程静电。

Result: ECENet 在 MD22 基准上达到当前最优水平；在 SPICE-MACE-OFF 数据集训练时位于精度-成本帕累托前沿；结合潜在 Ewald 求和后，能准确预测分子偶极矩及液态水的红外光谱。

Conclusion: ECENet 架构表明，等变边中心表示可作为等变 MLIPs 高效且具物理表达力的基础。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have emerged as a promising alternative to classical force fields and first-principles theory for predicting the properties of chemical and material systems. Many MLIPs are graph neural networks with O(3)-equivariant features, whose accuracy comes at substantial computational cost. Here we introduce the edge cluster expansion (ECE), an analogue of the atomic cluster expansion in which the environment is expanded around edges between atom pairs rather than single atoms, and build upon it to develop the line-graph neural network ECENet. ECENet uses O(2)-equivariant features that persist on the edges between atoms, giving it natural access to O(2) operations that are cheaper and less restrictive than their O(3) counterparts. ECENet performs at the state of the art on the MD22 benchmark and lies on the accuracy-cost Pareto frontier when trained on the SPICE-MACE-OFF dataset. Furthermore, with long-range electrostatics implemented via latent Ewald summation, ECENet accurately predicts molecular dipole moments and the infrared spectrum of liquid water. The frontier ECENet architecture establishes equivariant edge-centered representations as an efficient and physically expressive foundation for equivariant MLIPs.

</details>


### [64] [Attosecond charge migration timescales are dominated by transition dipoles, not correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23615)
*Km Akanksha Dubey,Ofer Neufeld*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文用含时密度泛函理论研究了BrC₄H分子中的阿秒电荷迁移（CM），发现空穴矩的主频近乎普适不依赖条件，而时标随化学环境与关联程度变化，并可由分子偶极的"选择定则"式滤波解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷迁移（CM）是太阳能转换、光合作用和辐射损伤中的关键超快过程，但对其基本物理化学机制、电子关联的作用以及决定CM时标的化学因素理解不足，尤其缺乏对关联效应和时标来源的深入认识。

Method: 采用从头算含时密度泛函理论（ab-initio TDDFT），在基准分子BrC₄H中研究CM；系统地改变初始条件、电子与结构层面条件，并使用不同电子关联程度的理论；同时构建半解析理论，在阳离子参考系中重构空穴矩。

Result: 发现空穴矩的主频（与实验可观测量相关）大致独立于初始条件、化学条件和关联程度，呈现普适行为；但空穴密度演化的时标随化学条件和关联水平而变化。半解析重构表明，阿秒CM时标由分子偶极决定，其作用类似光学选择定则，滤波出特定频率响应。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了CM物理的关键机制，为解释阿秒实验提供了重要见解，并指出可通过调控跃迁偶极来调控CM时标。

Abstract: Attosecond charge migration (CM) is an ultrafast process occurring when a molecule is irradiated by ultrashort laser pulses, creating a localized hole. The hole propagates rapidly through the molecule, generating electric currents and transferring charge across the molecular backbone. CM is a key ingredient in solar energy conversion, photosynthesis, and radiation damage. Despite its importance and intensive research, the fundamental physical and chemical mechanisms of CM remain not fully understood. Especially, a deeper insight into the role correlations play in the dynamics, and which chemical attributes determine CM timescales, is needed. Here we study with \textit{ab-initio} time-dependent density functional theory CM in the benchmark molecule, BrC$_4$H. We thoroughly explore CM under different initial conditions at the electronic and structural levels, including with theories of varying degrees of electronic correlations. We uncover a universal behavior where the hole moment (connecting to experimental observables) dominant frequency is roughly independent of all of these characteristics. In contrast, the timescales of the hole density evolution do vary with the chemical conditions and level of correlations. Employing a semi-analytical theory that reconstructs the hole moments in the cationic reference frame, we show that the attosecond timescale of CM is determined by molecular dipoles that filter out specific frequency responses with an analogy to optical selection rules. Our results provide essential insight into CM physics, which should be useful for interpreting attosecond experiments and engineering CM timescales by tailoring transition dipoles.

</details>


### [65] [AI-assisted analytical theory in soft matter and multiphysics: new results and lessons from diffusiophoresis and bipolar membranes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23930)
*Ankur Gupta*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一套借助AI推进软物质与多物理场解析理论的框架，含"诚实规则"、聚焦关键文献、事先设定校验、单任务日志与人类决策；应用于双电层任意电解质数与任意德拜长度下球形颗粒的扩散泳，以及双极膜I-V曲线（含过极限电流、不含水解离动力学）解析描述，两难题数天完成而传统需数月至一年，并警示避免认知卸载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软物质与多物理场解析理论推导通常耗时数月甚至一年，AI工具虽具潜力，但缺乏规范流程易导致错误传播、认知卸载与结果不可读，因此需要一套强调人类审核与可追溯性的研究框架。

Method: 框架包含五项要点：(i) 设定"诚实规则"；(ii) 聚焦少数用于构建目标问题的关键文献；(iii) 事先指定校验项；(iv) 每会话仅执行一个任务，并维护任务、校验、修正与会话的连续日志以防错误在项目中传播；(v) 所有决策由人类批准，AI主要承担执行。随后将该框架应用于两个独立问题。

Result: 在两个问题上生成新知识：(i) 存在任意数量电解质、任意德拜长度时球形颗粒的扩散泳；(ii) 不含水解离动力学的双极膜I-V曲线（含过极限电流）解析描述。两问题均为高阶分析，传统各需数月至一年，本框架下仅数天完成。

Conclusion: AI辅助显著提升理论推导效率，但须谨慎规避认知卸载，确保结果转化为人类可理解的洞见而非仅报告新数学公式；AI使用被透明呈现，并简要讨论了研究生教育中整合AI工具的思路。

Abstract: We present a framework to advance analytical theory in the area of soft matter and multiphysics using assistance from AI. The framework emphasizes (i) setting "honesty rules", (ii) focusing on a few key papers that are used to formulate a problem of interest, (iii) specifying checks a priori, (iv) using only one task per session and keeping a running log of tasks, checks, corrections, and sessions so that a mistake is not propagated through a project, and (v) ensuring all decisions are approved by humans while AI focuses primarily on execution. The framework is applied to two distinct problems to create new knowledge, including (i) diffusiophoresis of a spherical particle in the presence of an arbitrary number of electrolytes at arbitrary Debye lengths, and (ii) analytical description of I-V curves, including overlimiting current, in bipolar membranes without including the water dissociation kinetics. Both of these problems are advanced analyses and would each typically require several months to a year's worth of effort, but were solved in a matter of a few days. However, the increased efficiency should be cautiously navigated to avoid cognitive offloading and ensure that AI-assisted results are used to extract human-readable insights instead of just reporting a new mathematical formula. The usage of AI is transparently presented. Finally, we also share brief thoughts on how to integrate AI tools in theoretical research during graduate education.

</details>


### [66] [Benchmarking higher-ranking multipoles and polarizability tensors for small molecular systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24606)
*Bruno V. von Bruening,Alston J. Misquitta*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提供73个小分子的CCSD(T)分子偶极矩、四极矩、偶极-偶极与四极-四极极化率参考值，系统评估HF、MP2、CCSD及多种DFT方法的表现，并考察同时描述这些性质所需基组水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质决定分子间相互作用及与外场的相互作用，构建高精度分子间相互作用模型需超越领头阶（偶极）项，因此需要准确的偶极、四极及高阶极化率参考数据与方法评估。

Method: 为73个非自旋极化小分子生成CCSD(T)参考值，涵盖分子偶极矩、四极矩、偶极-偶极和四极-四极极化率；以此对HF、MP2、CCSD及多种密度泛函方法进行整体性能分析，并系统考察同时描述全部性质所需的基组水平。

Result: 表现最佳的泛函是渐近校正杂化GGA的B97-3-AC，PBE0-AC紧随其后；现代meta-GGA和range-separated泛函表现不稳定，在四极性质上误差较大。对DFT方法，Jensen aug-pcseg-2基组是高效替代；对相关参考值，第三行元素受益于核极化，且四极-四极极化率必须使用双增广基组。

Conclusion: 四极性质对泛函选择敏感，现代meta-GGA与range-separated泛函存在较大误差，该结果对基于DFT的对称适配微扰论等分子间相互作用方法以及机器学习数据集的性质与相互作用质量具有直接影响。

Abstract: Molecular properties govern how molecules interact with one another or external fields. Accurate molecular properties are essential for constructing intermolecular interaction models, and to achieve high accuracy we need to go beyond leading-order (dipolar) terms.
  This work presents CCSD(T) references for 73 small non-spin-polarized molecules for molecular dipole and quadrupole moments, and dipole-dipole and quadrupole-quadrupole polarizabilities. Using these results, we provide a holistic performance analysis of molecular properties computed with HF, MP2, CCSD, and a wide range of density-functional methods. Additionally, we investigate in detail what levels of basis sets are required to simultaneously describe all properties in the data set.
  The best-performing density functionals for all four properties are the asymptotically cor- rected hybrid GGAs B97-3-AC, closely followed by PBE0-AC. Surprisingly, modern meta- GGAs and range-separated functionals perform inconsistently, with large errors in the quadrupo- lar properties. These results have direct implications for methods for intermolecular interac- tions, such as symmetry-adapted perturbation theory based on density functional theory, or for the quality of properties and interactions in machine-learning datasets.
  Finally, for DFT methods, the Jensen aug-pcseg-2 basis set is a computationally efficient alternative to more established basis sets, but for generating correlated references, third-row elements benefit from core polarization, and double augmentation is strictly necessary for quadrupole-quadrupole polarizabilities.

</details>

### [67] [Generalized Multimodal Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22107)
*Huizi Cui,Zongbo Han,Chenggong Ding,Naichuan Xiao,Jialong Yang,Jingdong Chen,Guangyu Wang,Qinghua Hu,Changqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于大规模合成多模态数据生成的通用多模态基础模型（Generalized Multimodal Foundation Model），可处理任意模态组合与任意预测任务，无需任务特定适配，在18个真实数据集、12种模态、11类任务上达到与专用模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态融合模型部署后仅能处理预定义模态（如视觉、文本、音频）和单一任务，难以快速适应新的下游应用。因此作者提出一个自然却激进的问题：是否存在可适用于任意模态组合和任意预测任务的通用多模态融合模型。作者认为统一的多模态融合模型不应依赖特定模态，而应编码可迁移的多模态关联模式。

Method: 提出一种简单有效的学习范式，基于在大规模合成多模态数据集上训练，该数据集具有多样化的因果结构，形式化刻画真实世界多模态数据的生成过程。在此框架上构建通用多模态基础模型（generalized multimodal foundation model）。通过构建具有多样化关联模式的大规模合成多模态数据集，模型在训练中编码可迁移的多模态关联，并在推理时通过上下文示例（in-context examples）激活合适的关联。

Result: 在18个真实世界数据集、涵盖12种模态和11类预测任务上的大量实验表明，该模型无需任务特定适配即达到与专用模型相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了不依赖特定模态、通过合成数据学习可迁移多模态关联的通用多模态基础模型是可行的，为跨模态、跨任务的统一多模态融合提供了新范式。

Abstract: Making prediction with multimodal data is widely used in diverse scenarios. Existing multimodal fusion models, once deployed, can only handle predefined modalities (e.g., vision, text and audio) and single tasks, making it difficult to quickly adapt to new downstream applications. Therefore, a natural yet rather aggressive question arises, whether there exists a general multimodal fusion model that can be applied to arbitrary modality combinations and arbitrary prediction tasks. We argue that a unified multimodal fusion model should not depend on specific modalities and instead encode transferable patterns of multimodal correlation. To this end, we propose a simple and effective learning paradigm based on training over the generation of large-scale synthetic multimodal datasets with diverse causal structures that formally characterize the generative processes of multimodal data in real world. Building on this framework, we propose the generalized multimodal foundation model, a unified foundation model for generalized multimodal learning. By constructing large-scale synthetic multimodal datasets with diverse correlation patterns, our model encodes transferable multimodal correlations during training and activates appropriate associations through in-context examples during inference. Extensive experiments on 18 real-world datasets spanning 12 modalities and 11 prediction tasks demonstrate that our model achieves competitive performance with specialized models without task-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [68] [Helix-FNO: Spectral-Domain Operator Learning Coupled with a High-Fidelity Mechanistic Model for Fast Surrogate Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22129)
*Jiabao Zhao,Chuwei Wang,Jinxi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Helix-FNO，一种教师-学生架构，将32状态机理教师模型与傅里叶神经算子（FNO）学生模型耦合，学习解算子而非单一解，实现毫秒级推理，比机理教师快三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全尺度处理过程的机理仿真模型虽可外推、可信，但运行速度过慢，无法满足现代决策引擎5分钟决策节奏下成千上万次前向评估的需求。常规代理建模往往只针对单一配置学习单一解，对新的进水工况、控制设置或厂区布局泛化能力差。

Method: 采用教师-学生架构：教师为32状态机理模型，通过拉丁超立方和基于不确定性的主动学习构建高保真输入场-解配对数据集，覆盖边界和过载工况；学生为FNO，在谱域学习解算子本身。文中给出算子表述、谱卷积定义、加权蒸馏损失和主动学习准则，并分析了截断傅里叶展开相对于参数化解流形光滑性的近似误差。

Result: 算例研究将 Helix-FNO 与物理信息网络、数据驱动循环代理在精度、数据集效率和推理延迟方面对比，并置于速度-精度帕累托前沿。所得算子推理为毫秒级，比机理教师快三个数量级。

Conclusion: Helix-FNO 通过学习解算子实现毫秒级推理，具备大规模候选筛选与在线决策支持所需的能力，为全尺度处理过程的快速决策提供可行途径。

Abstract: Mechanistic simulation models of full-scale treatment processes remain the only trustworthy, extrapolative description of the underlying physico-chemical dynamics, yet their runtime is far too slow to support the thousands of forward evaluations that a modern decision engine requires at a 5-minute decision cadence. The standard remedy-surrogate modelling-often produces a network that learns a single solution for a single configuration, so it generalises poorly to new influent profiles, control settings or plant layouts. This paper presents Helix-FNO, a teacher-student architecture that couples a thirty-two-state mechanistic teacher with a Fourier neural operator (FNO) student. The teacher supplies a high-fidelity dataset of input-field-to-solution pairs, curated by Latin-hypercube and uncertainty-based active learning to cover the boundary and overload regimes that matter in practice; the student learns, in the spectral domain, the solution operator itself rather than any single solution, thereby moving from learning one instance to learning an entire family of equations. We give the operator formulation, the spectral convolution definition, the weighted distillation loss and the active-learning criterion, and we analyse the approximation error of a truncated Fourier expansion with respect to the smoothness of the parametric solution manifold. An illustrative study compares Helix-FNO against a physics-informed network and a data-driven recurrent surrogate on accuracy, dataset efficiency and inference latency, and places the methods on a speed-accuracy Pareto front. The resulting operator is three orders of magnitude faster than the mechanistic teacher at millisecond inference, which is precisely the capability required for massive candidate screening and online decision support.

</details>


### [69] [Hierarchical Bayesian optimization of an aircraft-based multi-agent system-of-systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22130)
*Paul Saves,Thierry Lefebvre,Nathalie Bartoli,Jasper Bussemaker,Nikolaos Kalliatakis,Nabih Naeem,Prajwal Prakasha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层贝叶斯优化框架，利用高斯过程元建模处理SoS架构设计中的离散选择、条件依赖与异构变量，在有限仿真预算下提升搜索效率与鲁棒性，并应用于野火抑制的多智能体航空系统案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂体系（SoS）架构设计依赖高保真多物理场、多学科仿真，但仿真计算成本高且存在评估失败风险，直接优化困难；传统代理模型方法在离散架构选择、条件依赖和异构设计变量方面能力不足。

Method: 构建分层贝叶斯优化框架，采用高斯过程元模型（meta-modeling）对体系问题中的离散架构选择、条件依赖关系及异构设计变量进行统一建模，在有限仿真预算下指导大规模、结构多样设计空间的探索。

Result: 相比传统基于代理模型的方法，分层建模显著提高了搜索效率与鲁棒性，能够在有限仿真预算下探索大型且结构多样的设计空间；在欧盟COLOSSUS项目中航空多智能体野火抑制案例上验证了方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架为体系架构设计及模型探索提供了可扩展方法，可为航空、可持续出行与韧性导向系统设计提供可迁移经验，是分层贝叶斯优化应用于真实体系问题的首批实践示范之一，兼具方法论与实践推进意义。

Abstract: Developing innovative system architectures increasingly relies on advanced modeling and optimization techniques to frame the architecting process and define the corresponding computational problems. For complex System-of-Systems (SoS), high-fidelity multiphysics and multidisciplinary simulations are essential for capturing detailed behaviors. However, their computational expense and the risk of evaluation failures make direct optimization challenging. To overcome these limitations, surrogate-based approaches, like Bayesian optimization, have emerged as effective tools for managing expensive, black-box simulation tasks. This work introduces a hierarchical Bayesian optimization framework that leverages Gaussian process meta-modeling to handle discrete architectural choices, conditional dependencies, and heterogeneous design variables inherent to SoS problems. Results show that the hierarchical formulation improves search efficiency and robustness compared to conventional surrogate-based methods, enabling the exploration of large and structurally diverse design spaces with limited simulation budgets.
  We apply the approach to an aircraft-based multi-agent system for wildfire suppression, a use case developed within the EU-funded COLOSSUS project that illustrates how SoS principles can coordinate heterogeneous aerial platforms with complementary roles, supporting both sustainable mobility and emergency response missions. Our framework provides a scalable methodology for SoS architecting and model exploration, offering transferable insights for applications in aviation, sustainable mobility, and resilience-oriented system design. By combining hierarchical representations with surrogate-based optimization, this work is among the first practical demonstrations of hierarchical Bayesian optimization applied to real-world SoS problems, advancing both methodology and practice.

</details>


### [70] [GRRR: The Geometry of Reshaping, Rotation, and Routing in Decoder LLM post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22146)
*Jianing Qi,Hao Tang,Zhigang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究将后训练权重更新分解到预训练权重SVD框架中，发现对角分量通常可被移除而不损失大部分收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究后训练（SFT与RL）如何相对预训练权重改变大语言模型参数，理解收益来源的几何机制。

Method: 在12条后训练链（SFT与RL）中，将每次权重更新表达在预训练矩阵的SVD框架下，分解为三个几何上不同的分量：对角值（重塑奇异值）、非对角值（旋转预训练输入与输出方向的耦合）、零空间值（在原始非零SVD核心之外的路由）。

Result: 在数学评测套件上，移除对角分量通常能保留后训练带来的大部分收益。

Conclusion: 后训练收益主要来自对预训练路径的重构与扩展，而非显著改变预训练模型的奇异值。

Abstract: We study how post-training changes the weights of Large Language Models (LLMs) relative to their pretrained weights. Across 12 post-training chains with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), we express each weight update in the pretrained matrix's singular value decomposition (SVD) frame. This decomposition separates the changes of three geometrically distinct components: diagonal values, which reshapes singular values; off-diagonal values, which rotates the coupling between pretrained input and output directions; and null-space values, which routes outside the matrix's original nonzero SVD core. On a math evaluation suite, we find that removing the diagonal component usually preserves most of the gains from post-training. These results suggest that post-training gains are carried primarily by reconfiguring and extending pretrained pathways rather than by substantially changing singular values of pre-trained models.

</details>


### [71] [StepKV: Step-Aware KV Cache Compression for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22158)
*Boyu Feng,Jiahong Liu,Yifan Li,Wenhao Yu,Zexuan Qiu,Yuliang Sun,Ming Shen,Xiang Li,Quanyu Dai,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StepKV提出以推理步骤为一等保留单元的KV缓存压缩方法，结合步骤效用与token级显著度对可剪枝token全局排序，在多步智能体推理中低预算下保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步LLM智能体中，查询会扩展为包含推理、工具交互与检索观测的长轨迹，KV缓存随上下文线性增长，存储与解码开销大。现有剪枝方法将缓存视为扁平token流，按近因或注意力显著度排序，导致压缩单元（token）与推理单元（步骤）不匹配：早期观测或中间决策可能近期注意力低但对后续证据综合至关重要，从而出现"推理连续性中断"。

Method: StepKV将推理步骤视为一等保留单元：把缓存条目关联到其生成步骤，从轨迹派生信号估计步骤效用，并将该效用与token级显著度结合；融合后的分数对可剪枝token进行全局排序，在目标预算下保留得分最高的条目。

Result: 在多跳问答与长时程网页推理任务上，StepKV在低KV预算下维持精度，而token级基线显著退化，提供了更稳健的效率-精度权衡。

Conclusion: StepKV为智能体KV缓存压缩提供了以步骤为中心的视角，通过联合考虑token级与推理步骤级信息，缓解推理连续性中断问题，适用于多步智能体推理。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) caching is essential for efficient autoregressive large language model (LLM) inference, but the cache grows linearly with context length, increasing storage and decoding costs. KV cache compression mitigates this cost by retaining only a subset of cached tokens. This challenge is particularly important for multi-step LLM agents, where a query expands into trajectories of reasoning, tool interactions, and retrieved observations. Existing pruning methods typically treat the cache as a flat token stream and rank tokens by recency or attention saliency. This creates a mismatch between the unit of compression and the unit of reasoning: token-level pruning removes individual entries, whereas useful information in multi-step agents is often organized into reasoning steps with uneven and delayed importance. Consequently, an early observation or intermediate decision may receive little recent attention yet remain essential for later evidence synthesis. We term this failure mode Reasoning Continuity Disruption.These observations motivate KV cache compression that jointly considers token- and reasoning-step-level information. StepKV addresses this goal by treating reasoning steps as first-class retention units. It associates cache entries with their generating steps, estimates step utility from trajectory-derived signals, and combines this utility with token-level saliency. The resulting scores globally rank prunable tokens, from which StepKV retains the top-scoring entries under a target budget. StepKV thus provides a step-centric perspective for agent KV cache compression. Across multi-hop QA and long-horizon web reasoning tasks, StepKV sustains accuracy under low KV budgets where token-level baselines degrade sharply, offering a more robust efficiency-accuracy trade-off for multi-step agent inference.

</details>


### [72] [Clustering-Based Collective Anomaly Detection in IoT Systems: A Graph Neural Network Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22166)
*Dalila Khettaf,Djamel Djenouri,Zeinab Rezaeifar,Youcef Djenouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无监督图集体异常检测框架UGCAD，利用变分图自编码器学习图表示以增强聚类，实现IoT网络流量中的集体异常检测，在CICIoT2023和ToN-IoT数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT设备广泛部署导致网络流量激增，恶意攻击机会增多，对异常检测需求上升。现有研究多聚焦点异常，集体异常检测（CAD）研究不足，尤其在无标签、无组信息场景下缺乏有效方法。

Method: UGCAD基于图结构数据，无需组标签或成员先验知识。采用变分图自编码器（VGAE）学习图表示，用于增强聚类算法以实现节点分组；先聚合并精炼被识别为正常的簇，再应用异常评分检测集体异常。

Result: 在CICIoT2023和ToN-IoT网络数据集上的大量实验表明，UGCAD在聚类和集体异常检测方面均有效；与多种传统及最先进的基于聚类的CAD方法对比，UGCAD在准确检测集体异常方面更具优势。

Conclusion: UGCAD为IoT网络流量中的无监督集体异常检测提供了有效方案，弥补了集体异常检测研究不足，并在多个数据集上优于现有基于聚类的方法。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Internet of Things (IoT) technology has led to the widespread deployment of smart, interconnected devices across a range of domains. However, this expansion has also resulted in a substantial increase in network traffic, creating more opportunities for malicious actors to launch cyberattacks and compromise sensitive information, thereby increasing the need for effective anomaly detection. The state-of-the-art in anomaly detection has predominantly focused on point anomalies. In contrast, the detection of collective anomalies remains relatively under-explored in the literature. In this paper, we introduce Unsupervised Graph Collective Anomaly Detection (UGCAD), a novel frame- work designed to identify collective anomalies in IoT network traffic. Unlike many existing methods, UGCAD operates on graph-structured data without any prior knowledge of group labels or membership. It leverages a variational graph autoencoder (VGAE) to learn the graph representation, which is subsequently used to enhance a clustering algorithm for effective grouping of nodes. To detect collective anomalies, clusters identified as normal are first aggregated and refined, after which anomaly scores are applied to detect collective anomalies. Extensive experiments conducted on the CICIoT2023 and ToN-IoT network datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of UGCAD in both clustering and collective anomaly detection (CAD). Furthermore, comparative evaluations against several traditional and state-of-the-art clustering-based CAD approaches confirm the superiority of UGCAD in accurately detecting collective anomalies.

</details>


### [73] [Role-Aware Morgan Fingerprints for Reaction Yield Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22167)
*Chinmay Mirji,Prashant Shekhar,Foram Madiyar,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MFP方法，用角色感知Morgan指纹加变换敏感差分特征构建固定长度反应描述符，经前馈网络回归预测产率，在两个基准上精度接近SOTA且训练快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反应产率预测方法（图神经网络、Transformer编码反应SMILES）存在预处理开销大、输入格式不一致时易失效的问题，亟需高效且稳健的方案。

Method: 对每个反应组分计算计数式循环指纹，按化学角色（反应物、试剂、产物）聚合，再与变换敏感的差分特征结合成固定长度反应描述符，输入前馈神经回归器；并进行复杂度分析与指纹半径、向量长度消融实验。

Result: 在Suzuki-Miyaura上R²=0.878、Buchwald-Hartwig上R²=0.969，训练速度比图/Transformer方法快一个数量级；复杂度分析表明表示成本被折叠进一次性预处理；半径2、nBits=2048在精度、速度与跨划分稳定性上最优。

Conclusion: MFP是有效、可复现且高效的反应产率预测基线方法。

Abstract: Predicting reaction yield from molecular structure and reaction context can cut experimental trial-and-error and speed up condition screening in synthetic chemistry. Recent methods for this task use learned representations such as graph neural networks or Transformer encoders over reaction SMILES (Simplified Molecular Input Line Entry System), but these approaches carry heavy preprocessing overhead and can break when input formatting is inconsistent. We propose MFP, a reaction yield prediction method built on role-aware Morgan fingerprints where count-based circular fingerprints are computed for each reaction component, aggregated by chemical role (reactant, reagent, product), and combined with transformation-sensitive difference features into a fixed-length reaction descriptor fed to a feed-forward neural regressor. We test MFP against state of the art methods such as YieldBERT (with and without data augmentation) and GNAN (graph neural network) on the Suzuki-Miyaura and Buchwald-Hartwig benchmarks using a shared preprocessing and evaluation protocol. MFP reaches R2 = 0.878 on Suzuki-Miyaura and R2 = 0.969 on Buchwald-Hartwig while training an order of magnitude faster than graph- or Transformer-based alternatives. A formal complexity analysis confirms that MFP folds all representation cost into a one-time preprocessing step, removing the per-epoch message-passing overhead that graph methods carry. An ablation over fingerprint radius and folded vector length shows that radius-2 representations at nBits =2048 give the best balance of accuracy, speed, and cross-split stability on both datasets. These results establish MFP as an effective, reproducible, and efficient baseline for reaction yield prediction.

</details>


### [74] [Multiple latent orderings better predict language model preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22170)
*Aviral Chawla,William H. W. Thompson,Jean-Gabriel Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出LLM偏好中的非传递性并非采样噪声，而是多个内部一致的潜在排序的聚合结果，并通过噪声增强混合Bradley-Terry模型验证了这一观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型常被用于价值判断与选择，其观察到的偏好常出现非传递性（如偏好A>B、B>C，却偏好C>A）。现有工作将这种不一致性视为单一潜在排序周围的采样噪声，但该解释可能不足，作者希望重新解释非传递性的来源。

Method: 首先证明在任意单调链接函数下，观察到的非一致性无法由单一排序解释；随后提出噪声增强的混合Bradley-Terry（MBT）模型，从重复成对比较中推断潜在的偏好成分（多个内部一致的排序）。

Result: 在七个模型和四个任务上，排序的混合模型比单一效用模型更好地解释结构不一致性；发现聚合偏好常掩盖潜在的偏好异质性；在Moral Machine困境案例中，聚合排序不一致的模型仍可能共享潜在成分。

Conclusion: LLM反映的是多元偏好（plural preferences）。将对LLM偏好的对齐与评估流程视为单一函数，会有对不同用户可能以不同方式认可的多个一致排序进行平均的风险。

Abstract: Language models are frequently employed in settings where they are asked to make value judgments and choices. These observed choices often exhibit intransitivity: A model may prefer item $A$ to $B$ and $B$ to $C$, while also preferring $C$ to $A$. Existing work that models LLM preferences treats such inconsistencies as sampling noise around a single latent ordering. We instead propose that intransitivity reflects the aggregation of multiple latent, internally consistent orderings. We first show that observed inconsistencies cannot be explained by a single ordering under any monotone link function. We then introduce a noise-augmented mixture Bradley-Terry (MBT) model that infers latent preference components from repeated pairwise comparisons. Across seven models and four tasks, a mixture of orderings often explains structural inconsistencies better than single-utility models. We find that aggregate preferences often hide underlying preference heterogeneity. A case study on Moral Machine dilemmas shows that models which disagree on aggregate orderings can still share latent components. Together, these results suggest that LLMs reflect plural preferences. Alignment and evaluation pipelines that treat LLM preferences as a single function, therefore, risk averaging over coherent orderings that different users may endorse differently.

</details>


### [75] [EvoRank: LLM-Guided Evolution of Multi-Objective Learning-to-Rank Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22196)
*Rayhan Patel,Shabaz Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 EvoRank：LLM 引导的进化式自动化排序工程框架，可在多目标电商搜索中端到端发现可解释的 Learning-to-Rank 流水线，并给出预测其收益的 headroom gate。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化 LTR 流水线设计通常依赖人工试错或超参调优，难以同时优化相关性、转化与营收等多目标；同时，小规模验证集上的提升常常无法迁移到留出数据，导致投入大量 LLM 成本却收益存疑。作者希望提供可复现的自主进化流程，并在花钱之前判断该流程是否值得做。

Method: EvoRank 采用 LLM 引导的进化循环，搜索完整 LTR 流水线空间（特征、模型、损失、集成）。在 Expedia ICDM 2013 数据上以相关性、转化、营收为竞争目标，三次独立运行均在 50 轮内（约 10 美元）收敛。第一轮 campaign 仅进化训练目标，配合 transfer audit 在留出数据上重评分，并引入诊断反馈与领域知识注入作对照。

Result: 三条可解释流水线在 6 万留出 query 上击败 Optuna 调参的 LambdaMART，优势在全量数据下仍保持，位列原竞赛前 6%。transfer audit 显示初轮增益几乎完全来自 fitness noise；种子领域知识与更丰富诊断反馈均未改变可迁移性。核心决定量为搜索空间 headroom 与 fitness noise 之比，可提前量化并打包为 headroom gate。

Conclusion: EvoRank 证明 LLM 引导进化可用于端到端多目标排序流水线发现，并通过 headroom gate 在 LLM 开销发生前预测收益是否成立；同时开源系统、审计工具与失败模式 catalog 及防护规则，便于团队迁移到自有排序栈。

Abstract: We present EvoRank, an open autonomous ranking engineer: an LLM-guided evolutionary loop that discovers complete Learning-to-Rank pipelines (features, models, losses, ensembles) for multi-objective e-commerce search. On the Expedia ICDM 2013 dataset, with relevance, conversion, and revenue as competing objectives, three independent runs each converge within 50 iterations (about ten dollars) on interpretable pipelines that beat an Optuna-tuned LambdaMART on 60k held-out queries, an advantage that persists at full data scale and places in the top 6 percent of the original competition. A first campaign, evolving only training objectives, builds the central design rule: it appeared to work on its selection fold (the small dataset it uses to pick winners) while a transfer audit, re-scoring winners on held-out data, showed the gains were almost entirely fitness noise (the randomness of its own scoring), and neither seeded domain knowledge nor richer diagnostic feedback changed what transferred. The deciding quantity is measurable in advance: search-space headroom relative to fitness noise. We package this as a headroom gate that predicts, before any LLM spend, whether the loop will pay off, and we release the system, the auditing tools, and a catalog of failure modes with their guardrails, so teams can apply the procedure to their own ranking stacks.

</details>


### [76] [SCALE: Simulation-Calibrated Amortized Learning for Energy Materials (A hybrid architecture connecting deterministic modeling, real-world data, and transformer-scale inference for accelerated energy-materials discovery)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22233)
*Kuan Huang,Bo Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCALE——一种物理奠基、真实数据校准的摊销学习架构，将确定性科学算子、实验校准、校准标签生成与Transformer推理相连，用于加速能源材料发现；以固态金属氢化物储氢容量预测为例验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源系统在安全性、可负担性、韧性与可持续性方面面临多重压力，亟需更快速地发现可部署的能源材料。然而高成本机理计算与实验证据难以直接支撑百万级候选材料的高通量筛选。

Method: SCALE将高成本机理计算与实测证据转化为可复用的学习模型，支持快速筛选、排序、逆向设计与主动学习。框架提出十类基于方法的应用范式。在金属氢化物储氢容量预测中，以381个ML-HydPARK实测容量锚点校准氢化物相平衡容量算子，生成5,000个候选-条件-原型教师标签；采用晶体学锚定的周期图表示保留原子位点、周期邻居关系与局部金属环境；构建含290万参数的边偏置图Transformer进行代理拟合。

Result: 五折代理保真度达MAE 0.0582 wt% H2、RMSE 0.0833 wt% H2、R²=0.9927、Pearson r=0.9963。事后注意力分析显示模型在无化学分组标签监督下自发学到与既有氢化物化学一致的元素分组及金属-金属关系。训练后可将百万候选评估从重复确定性工作流执行转为批量学习推理，单候选筛选成本降低约10^7–10^8倍，同时保留与模拟和实验证据的关联。

Conclusion: SCALE通过物理算子校准与摊销学习相结合，实现了高精度、低成本、可追溯的能源材料快速筛选与设计，展示了将高成本机理计算和实验证据转化为可复用推理模型的可行性。

Abstract: Energy systems face converging pressures for security, affordability, resilience, and sustainability, creating a need for faster discovery of deployable energy materials. Here we introduce SCALE (Simulation-Calibrated Amortized Learning for Energy Materials), a physics-grounded, real-world-data-calibrated learning architecture that connects deterministic scientific operators, experimental calibration, expanded calibrated label generation, and transformer-scale inference. SCALE converts selected high-cost mechanistic computation and measured evidence into reusable models for rapid screening, ranking, inverse design, and active learning. We formulate the framework, identify ten method-based application regimes, and demonstrate SCALE for solid-state metal-hydride hydrogen-storage capacity prediction. In this implementation, a hydride phase-equilibrium capacity operator is calibrated against 381 measured ML-HydPARK capacity anchors and used to generate 5,000 candidate-condition-prototype teacher labels. A crystallographically anchored periodic-graph representation preserves atomic sites, periodic neighbor relationships, and local metal environments absent from formula-only encodings. An edge-biased graph transformer with 2.90 million parameters reproduces calibrated teacher labels with five-fold surrogate fidelity of MAE 0.0582 wt% H2, RMSE 0.0833 wt% H2, R2 = 0.9927, and Pearson r = 0.9963. Post hoc attention analysis suggests that SCALE learns chemically organized element groupings and metal-metal relationships consistent with established hydride chemistry, without chemistry-group labels as supervision. Once trained, SCALE shifts million-candidate evaluation from repeated deterministic workflow execution to batched learned inference, reducing per-candidate screening cost by approximately 10^7-10^8 while retaining links to simulation and experimental evidence.

</details>


### [77] [Toollery: Scaling LLM Agents to Thousands of Skills and Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22218)
*Xiangxi Tian,Ran Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Toollery：无需训练的候选压缩框架，通过为技能/工具规格生成用户意图查询并构建检索索引，将大规模能力库的在线LLM选择压缩到紧凑的top-k候选集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体面对数百至数万个技能、工具和API时，全库提示成本高、速度慢且可靠性下降：候选增加会提升提示token与延迟，更长候选列表还引入更多干扰项，影响LLM选择。

Method: Toollery采用无需训练的方式，借鉴文档侧查询扩展，从每个技能/工具规格生成用户意图查询，并构建检索索引，将真实用户请求映射为紧凑候选集，再交由LLM最终决策；同时把高层技能与原子工具统一视为可选择的“能力”，兼容技能库与工具注册表。

Result: 在约79K能力的SkillRouter基准、包含440多个原子工具的BFCL-V4，以及220多个工具上的3396条专有智能座舱请求上评估。Toollery将在线选择限制在紧凑top-k候选集内，并相较普通规格检索提升召回；固定top-10预算下，在座舱数据集提升端到端选择效果，在BFCL-V4保持可比的AST Accuracy。

Conclusion: Toollery是适用于大规模且不断演化的智能体能力库的实用候选压缩框架；其质量与成本收益依赖工作负载覆盖度与提供方缓存。

Abstract: As LLM agents are exposed to hundreds to tens of thousands of skills, tools, and API functions, full-library prompting becomes costly, slow, and less reliable: each added candidate increases prompt tokens and latency, while longer candidate lists introduce more distractors for LLM selection. We present \textbf{Toollery}, a training-free candidate-compression framework for scalable LLM skill/tool selection. Following established document-side query expansion, Toollery generates user-intent queries from each skill/tool specification and builds a retrieval index that maps real user requests to compact candidate sets before final LLM decision-making. By treating high-level skills and atomic tools as selectable capabilities, Toollery can be applied to both skill libraries and tool registries. We evaluate Toollery on the roughly 79K-capability SkillRouter benchmark, BFCL-V4 with over 440 atomic tools, and 3,396 proprietary smart-cockpit requests over 220 tools. Across these settings, Toollery keeps online selection bounded to a compact top-$k$ candidate set and improves recall over ordinary specification retrieval. At a fixed top-10 budget, Toollery improves end-to-end selection on the cockpit dataset, and maintains comparable AST Accuracy on BFCL-V4. These results support Toollery as a practical candidate-compression framework for large and evolving agent capability libraries, while showing that quality and cost gains depend on workload coverage and provider caching.

</details>


### [78] [Measuring the Checker: Mutation Analysis for GPU-Kernel Benchmark Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22220)
*Mingzhe Du,Anh Tuan Luu,Dong Huang,See-Kiong Ng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出用变异分析（mutation analysis）作为 GPU 内核基准测试判定器（oracle）充分性度量：向 188 个 KernelBench 问题的已验证 CUDA 实现注入 10,303 个可编译故障（其中 7,384 个含独立 kill witness），任何测试协议按检出故障比例评分。官方 checker 确定性漏检 16.9% 的 witness 故障，精度类故障漏检率高达 78.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 生成 GPU Kernel 基准仅用少量随机输入和宽松浮点容差判正确性，却驱动排行榜与 RL 奖励；已有工作虽认同其薄弱并手工打补丁（加输入分布、fuzzing、收紧容差），但缺乏衡量补丁是否充分的量化手段。

Method: 以变异分析作为充分性指标：用确定性规则向已验证 CUDA 实现注入 10,303 个可编译故障，为 7,384 个故障构造独立 kill witness；按测试协议检出故障的比例评分；进一步分析容差盲带、输入激进性上限，审计 KernelBench-Verified 补丁增益来源，并在 kill matrix 上优化测试套件，跨 48 种整体架构统计盲区。

Result: 官方 checker 确定性漏检 16.9% 的 witness 故障，算术故障漏检 8.7%、精度故障漏检 78.6%；解释了漏检成因（随归约规模增长的容差盲带、由合法浮点方差决定的输入激进度上限）；审计出 KernelBench-Verified 增益为隐藏输入 +4.0 分、收紧容差 +4.5 分（原作者无法计算该拆分）；暴露某已发表 fuzzing 配方 107 次误拒正确内核；在 kill matrix 上优化两输入/问题达 98.0% 检出（留出 94.8%）；盲区随规模增大，集中于深层同构流水线；两个问题的官方参考实现对 fp64 违反基准自身容差，属不可裁判。

Conclusion: 变异分析可作为 kernel 基准 oracle 的可量化充分性度量，能解释、审计和优化现有检查与补丁，并揭示随规模增长的盲区与不可裁判问题；故障分类学比原始故障更能指导测试生成器。所有资源以 KernelBench-M 数据集发布。

Abstract: Benchmarks for LLM-generated GPU kernels decide correctness with a few random inputs and a loose floating-point tolerance, and their verdicts now feed leaderboards and reinforcement-learning rewards. Recent work agrees these checkers are weak and patches them by hand---extra input distributions, fuzzing recipes, tighter tolerances---with no way to \emph{measure} whether any patch suffices. We introduce mutation analysis as an adequacy metric for kernel-benchmark oracles: deterministic rules inject 10{,}303 compilable faults into verified CUDA implementations of 188 KernelBench problems, 7{,}384 of them with an independent kill witness; any test protocol is scored by the fraction it detects. The official check misses \textbf{one in six} witnessed faults (16.9%), deterministically, and the misses are skewed by family: 8.7% of arithmetic faults escape, but 78.6% of precision faults do. The metric explains why (a tolerance blind band growing with reduction size; a measured ceiling on input aggressiveness set by legitimate floating-point variance), audits the strongest existing patch (KernelBench-Verified's gain splits into $+4.0$ points from hidden inputs and $+4.5$ from tighter tolerance, a split its authors could not compute), and exposes a published fuzzing recipe that rejects \emph{correct} kernels 107 times. Optimizing suites over the kill matrix reaches 98.0% detection with two inputs per problem (94.8% held-out), and the measurement's fault taxonomy teaches a test generator more than the raw faults themselves. Across 48 whole architectures, the blindness grows with scale, concentrating in deep homogeneous pipelines, and two problems prove unrefereeable: their official references violate the benchmark's own tolerance against fp64. We release everything as \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/Elfsong/KernelBench-M}{KernelBench-M}.

</details>


### [79] [Universal Observatory Graphs for Distributed Sky Coverage and Artificial Intelligence Based Interplanetary Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22244)
*Mohammed Abdel Razek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"通用天文台图"(UOG)框架，将以太阳-行星L2点自主天文台为节点、深空通信链路为边建模为加权图，统一描述星际协同观测网络；用三种方法评估六节点（地球-海王星）网络瞬时覆盖，并将通信路由建模为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程、用表格型Q学习求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式星际天文观测需要统一的数学框架来同时刻画"观测覆盖"与"通信路由"两类问题；现有研究缺乏对太阳系尺度自主天文台网络的可复现建模与多目标路由基线，因此需要把节点几何覆盖能力与链路物理/运行指标纳入同一图表示。

Method: (1) 图建模：太阳-行星L2点自主天文台为带权图的节点，边赋以多目标物理与运行指标，包括行星际距离、通信时延、发射功率与链路可靠性；(2) 覆盖评估：以地球、火星、木星、土星、天王星、海王星构成六观测台配置，分别用20万方向Fibonacci球、200万方向固定种子蒙特卡洛、确定性球面积分三种方法独立计算瞬时天区覆盖；(3) 路由求解：将通信路由形式化为有限时域马尔可夫决策过程，用表格型Q学习求解，奖励函数在节点参与度与距离相关可靠性代理（正项）和距离、光行时延迟、距离平方发射功率代理（负项）之间做权衡。

Result: 三种覆盖计算方法结果一致：网络并集实现完全覆盖，六观测台交集覆盖率约0.43%，平均成对Jaccard相似度约24.96%。在四跳约束下全部41条可行简单路径中，Q学习得到的"地球-土星-天王星-海王星"路由同时具有最高折扣回报。

Conclusion: UOG为序列化覆盖评估与多目标路由提供了一个可复现的基线框架；含时历表、任务特定可见性、标定链路预算以及可扩展图策略（规模化图神经网络类策略）留作未来工作。

Abstract: This research proposes the Universal Observatory Graph (UOG), an AI-driven framework for distributed astronomical observation across the Solar System. The proposed architecture models autonomous observatories located at the Sun planet L2 Lagrange points as nodes in a weighted graph, while communication links are represented as graph edges characterized by multi-objective physical and operational metrics, including interplanetary distance, communication latency, transmission power, and link reliability. The resulting graph provides a unified mathematical representation of a cooperative interplanetary observatory network. This proposal examines a six-observatory Solar System configuration comprising Earth, Mars, Jupiter, Saturn, Uranus and Neptune. Instantaneous sky coverage is evaluated independently using a 200,000 direction Fibonacci sphere, a 2,000,000 direction fixed seed Monte Carlo calculation and deterministic spherical integration. All three methods yield complete network union coverage, approximately 0.43% complete six observatory intersection and approximately 24.96% mean pairwise Jaccard similarity under the adopted pointing model. Communication routing is subsequently formulated as a finite horizon Markov decision process and solved using tabular Q-learning. The reward balances node participation and a distance dependent reliability proxy against distance, light time latency and a distance squared transmission power proxy. The learned Earth-Saturn-Uranus-Neptune route is also the highest discounted return route among all 41 feasible simple paths under the four hop constraint. The framework provides a reproducible baseline for sequential coverage assessment and multi objective routing; time dependent ephemerides, mission specific visibility, calibrated link budgets and scalable graph policies remain future work.

</details>


### [80] [CALM: A Calibrated LLM Choice Network Framework for Activity-Based Traveler Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22252)
*Yezhou Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CALM是一个可复现的混合出行仿真框架，将可选LLM活动规划器与校准随机选择、共享网络反馈、记忆与习惯、类型化可行性检验及确定性离线回放集成，并在纽约市CMS数据上通过训练集专属校准、模块消融与受控压力测试验证生成式规划器的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多为出行方式分类器或仅生成日志（diary-only）的生成器，缺乏对生成模块的经验性、可复现基线评估，也未执行完整的闭环出行者日活动。作者希望提供一个可复现协议，将生成式规划器整合进出行仿真并对其各模块进行严格评估。

Method: CALM整合可选LLM活动规划器、校准随机选择、共享网络反馈、记忆与习惯机制、带类型的可行性检验以及确定性离线回放。基于2024年纽约市Citywide Mobility Survey（CMS）的110,691条七模式出行，按受访者划分为78,487条训练与32,204条留出样本。采用仅用训练集的替代方案特定常数（alternative-specific constant）校准，并进行匹配的在线LLM消融实验；同时构建天气、延误、票价与停车四类受控阶梯（ladders）干预。

Result: 仅用训练集校准使十个随机种子下的留出方式Jensen-Shannon散度均值从0.15599降至0.00394。匹配的在线LLM消融量化了聚合拟合、时间拟合、行为持续性与可行性之间的权衡；冻结的提示-响应对支持下游仿真的确定性回放。受控的天气、延误、票价与停车阶梯显示出一致且可解释的干预响应。

Conclusion: CALM提出了可复现协议，通过个体不重叠（person-disjoint）校准、匹配模块消融、受控压力测试与端到端可追溯性，为在出行仿真中整合和评估生成式规划器提供了方法论贡献。

Abstract: We present CALM, a reproducible hybrid framework that integrates an optional large language model (LLM) activity planner with calibrated stochastic choice, shared network feedback, memory and habit, typed feasibility checks, and deterministic offline replay. Unlike trip-mode classifiers or diary-only generators, CALM executes a closed traveler-day loop and evaluates each generative module against an empirical, reproducible baseline. On the 2024 New York City Citywide Mobility Survey (CMS), 110,691 seven-mode trips are split by respondent into 78,487 training and 32,204 holdout trips. Training-only alternative-specific constant calibration reduces mean holdout mode Jensen-Shannon divergence from 0.15599 to 0.00394 across ten seeds. A matched live-LLM ablation then quantifies trade-offs among aggregate fit, temporal fit, behavioral persistence, and feasibility, while frozen prompt-response pairs support deterministic replay of downstream simulation. Controlled weather, delay, fare, and parking ladders further demonstrate consistent and interpretable responses under intervention. CALM contributes a reproducible protocol for integrating and evaluating generative planners in traveler simulation through person-disjoint calibration, matched module ablation, controlled stress testing, and end-to-end traceability.

</details>


### [81] [CAMFT: Conflict-Aware Mergeable Fine-Tuning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22253)
*Jingang Zhou,Haiyang Guo,Yuan Ma,Han Zhu,Xu-Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CAMFT，一种冲突感知的可合并微调方法，将"可合并性"视为微调阶段塑造的属性，通过引导各任务在跨任务冲突更低的稀疏坐标上更新，从而兼顾训练效率与下游模型合并的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法多为对独立微调模型的后处理，忽视了训练阶段对跨任务兼容性的影响，导致微调后解决参数冲突本质上次优。因此需要让微调本身具备合并意识。

Method: CAMFT 将合并性视为微调过程中塑造的属性，而非事后求解的问题；其核心是引导每个任务在跨任务冲突较低的稀疏坐标上进行参数更新，使任务更新既高效又更易于下游合并。

Result: 大量实验表明，在多任务合并场景下 CAMFT 优于标准微调基线。

Conclusion: 训练阶段即引入冲突感知，可使任务适配兼具高效性与可合并性，为多任务模型整合提供了更优范式。

Abstract: Model merging has emerged as a promising paradigm for integrating multiple task-specific capabilities into a single large language model. However, existing methods predominantly focus on post-hoc processing of independently fine-tuned models, overlooking how the training phase itself impacts cross-task compatibility. Resolving parameter conflicts after fine-tuning is inherently sub-optimal. To address this, we propose CAMFT, a Conflict-Aware Mergeable Fine-Tuning method that makes task adaptation both efficient and mergeaware. CAMFT treats mergeability as a property shaped during fine-tuning, rather than only a problem to be solved after fine-tuning. By guiding each task to update sparse coordinates with lower cross-task conflict, CAMFT produces task updates that are efficient to train and more compatible for downstream model merging. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CAMFT outperforms standard finetuning baselines in multi-task merging scenarios. Codes are available at https://github.com/gyanchow/CAMFT-LLM.

</details>


### [82] [Strategy Accumulation and Guided Execution for Automated LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22257)
*Haoran Zhao,Wei Du,Dingwen Yang,Jixuan Huang,Junlin Shang,Lingyong Fang,Ya Guo,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAGE框架，通过构建可累积的经验库，使自动化微调搜索具备记忆能力，在九个未见任务上显著提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化微调系统是无状态的，每次搜索结束后会丢弃所发现的策略、数据集洞察与超参数结论，导致每个新任务都需从冷启动重复进行昂贵的搜索。因此需要一种能使自动微调搜索具备累积性的方法。

Method: SAGE为两阶段框架。第一阶段：多智能体流水线执行基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的探索，并行的蒸馏智能体提取任务特定探索记录与带置信度评分的跨任务洞察，共同构成结构化经验库。第二阶段：从该经验库中检索相关经验，并选择适用部分来指导新任务的训练。

Result: 在涵盖单类别与跨类别的九个未见任务上评估。单轮执行中，SAGE累积经验将相比基线的平均相对提升从3.2%提高到15.6%，比无经验库的相同流水线高出12.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 持久化的策略经验可为未见任务的自动化微调提供有效指导，验证了使自动微调搜索具备累积性的可行性。

Abstract: Producing task-specific large language models requires discovering effective training strategies through experimentation. Automated fine-tuning systems have made this experimentation feasible with far less manual effort. However, these systems are stateless: each search discards its discovered strategies, dataset insights, and hyperparameter findings once it ends. Every new task must then repeat this costly search from a cold start. To address this, we propose Strategy Accumulation and Guided Execution (SAGE), a two-stage framework that makes automated fine-tuning search cumulative. In the first stage, a multi-agent pipeline performs Monte Carlo Tree Search-based exploration. A parallel Distillation Agent extracts task-specific exploration records and confidence-scored cross-task insights, which together constitute a structured experience repository. In the second stage, SAGE retrieves relevant experience from this repository and selects what applies to guide training on the new task. We evaluate SAGE on nine unseen tasks spanning both single- and cross-category settings. In single-round execution, SAGE's accumulated experience raises the average relative improvement over baseline from 3.2% to 15.6%, a 12.4-percentage-point gain over the same pipeline without it. These results show that persistent strategy experience provides effective guidance for automated fine-tuning on unseen tasks.

</details>


### [83] [RS-Claw-Evolution: Environment-Feedback-Driven Evolution for Lightweight Remote Sensing Agents in Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22258)
*Kai Ouyang,Dongyang Hou,Liangtian Liu,Zeyuan Wang,Ziyu Li,Chengfu Liu,Zichao Tang,Xuezhi Cui,Shengwu Ouyang,Wentao Yang,Hanwen Yu,Haifeng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RS-Claw-Evolution框架，通过交互演化、经验演化和决策演化三阶段，利用环境反馈逐步提升轻量级遥感智能体的长时序任务能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级遥感智能体（基于紧凑语言模型）在多步交互任务中面临三大瓶颈：长时序状态丢失、环境反馈利用低效、优化信号稀疏。

Method: 三阶段环境反馈驱动框架：1）交互演化——用可执行代码控制观测、维护中间状态、降低上下文冗余；2）经验演化——故障感知轨迹生成结合错误轮掩码，从失败-恢复经验中学习而不模仿错误动作；3）决策演化——基于多维环境奖励的强化学习与轮级优势保护，优化工具使用行为并改善长序列信用分配。

Result: 在Earth-Bench上，基于Qwen3-4B优化后的智能体在自主规划模式达到65.9%准确率，超过未训练Qwen3-32B基线（43.8%）与DeepSeek-V3.1（60.8%），接近GPT-5（71.6%）。

Conclusion: 从环境反馈中学习可有效提升轻量级智能体性能，缩小其与大型模型在长时序遥感任务上的差距。

Abstract: Large language model-driven remote sensing (RS) agents offer a promising approach to automating geospatial analysis. However, lightweight RS agents based on compact language models struggle with multi-step interactive tasks due to loss of long-horizon states, inefficient environmental feedback utilization, and sparse optimization signals. We propose RS-Claw-Evolution, an environment-feedback-driven framework that progressively improves lightweight agents through three stages. Interaction evolution uses executable code to control observations, maintain intermediate states, and reduce context redundancy. Experience evolution combines failure-aware trajectory generation with error-turn masking to learn from informative failure-recovery experiences without imitating faulty actions. Decision evolution uses reinforcement learning with multi-dimensional environment rewards and turn-level advantage protection to optimize tool-use behaviors and improve credit assignment in long sequences. On Earth-Bench, the optimized Qwen3-4B-based agent achieves 65.9% accuracy in Autonomous Planning mode, outperforming the untrained Qwen3-32B baseline (43.8%) and DeepSeek-V3.1 (60.8%), while approaching GPT-5 (71.6%). These results demonstrate that learning from environmental feedback can improve lightweight agents and narrow their performance gap with larger models in long-horizon RS tasks.

</details>


### [84] [Resist, Update, Reject: Preference Optimization Installs a Prior-Dependent Reliability Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22359)
*Sen Yang,Yuen-Hei Yeung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 反谄媚优化目标对信源可靠性"不可辨识"，固定↔轻信前沿由目标本身决定；通过带可靠性阈值标注的偏好优化，可让模型学会依先验强度决定是否改答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐模型需同时满足三重契约：抵抗操控、采纳可靠来源、拒绝不可靠来源。既有反谄媚工作所优化的目标无法区分信源是否真正可靠，导致任何标量偏好组合都只形成单一的"顺从旋钮"，无法区分仅在陈述可靠性上不同的同模板证言。

Method: 构造阈值基准：来源给出相反答案并声明可靠性 r，正确行为是当且仅当 r 超过模型先验强度 p 时改答。采用均衡覆盖的偏好优化（含 DPO、IPO 等）进行训练，并设置不匹配变体、监督模仿等对照以定位成因。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 三个随机种子上，阈值 r* 随先验单调上升，决策准确率达 0.84，翻转曲线单调（Spearman 0.56），并泛化到未见可靠性值与留出表述方式，优先于角色声望。对照显示：不匹配变体同样安装 (0.80)，IPO 同样有效 (0.86)，监督模仿无效 (0.50)。确认性测试在 Llama-3.1-8B 上复现。

Conclusion: 可靠性可辨识性来自数据设计而非目标本身；成因是"基于可靠性标注覆盖的偏好优化"，而非配对方式、损失函数或模仿。该模型只对证言中陈述的可靠性敏感，而非独立审核记录，且该前沿是经验发现而非定理。

Abstract: An aligned model asked to hold its answer against a manipulative source must still update on a reliable one and reject an unreliable one: resistance, reliable-update, and unreliable-source rejection are one three-way contract, not three independent behaviors. We show the objective most anti-sycophancy work optimizes is non-identifying with respect to source reliability: because no preference label depends on whether a source is actually reliable, any scalar mixture of the arms traces a single deference dial, and no point separates two same-template testimonies differing only in stated reliability. This fixation$\leftrightarrow$gullibility frontier is a property of the objective, not any model. We make reliability identifiable through data: a threshold benchmark where a source asserts the opposite answer while stating its reliability $r$, and the correct action is to flip iff $r$ exceeds the model's prior strength $p$. Preference optimization over balanced coverage installs a prior-dependent reliability switch: across three seeds on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct the threshold $r^\star$ rises monotonically with the prior, decision accuracy reaches $0.84$ with a monotone flip curve (Spearman $0.56$), and the policy generalizes to unseen reliability values and a held-out notation, following stated reliability over role prestige. Three controls localize the cause: an unmatched variant installs the switch equally ($0.80$), a second preference optimizer (IPO) installs it just as well ($0.86$), whereas supervised imitation does not ($0.50$), so the cause is preference optimization over reliability-labeled coverage, not pairing, loss, or imitation. A confirmatory battery replicates the switch on a fresh test draw, bounds it honestly (it keys on reliability stated in the testimony, not a separately audited record), and transfers it to Llama-3.1-8B. The frontier is empirical, not a theorem.

</details>


### [85] [A Hybrid Attention Model Learning Unified Time-aware Patch Representation for Irregular Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22836)
*Zhihao Lin,Li Lin,Qi Zhang,Kaiwen Xia,Shuai Wang,Jialin Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合注意力模型统一时间感知patch表示，实现不规则多变量时间序列零样本预测，并构建30B观测预训练数据集VersaTSA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策常依赖不规则多变量时间序列（IMTS），存在观测间隔不一致、变量间异步采样及信息性缺失；现有时间序列基础模型（TSFM）或用插补引入虚假值，或用索引位置编码忽略连续时间，尚缺遵循原始IMTS模式的基础模型。此外，缺乏保留原生采样稀疏性的大规模预训练数据。

Method: 1) 时间感知patch编码：将patch内可变数量时间戳映射为固定维度嵌入，无需插补即可统一不规则patch表示；2) 时间偏置注意力：以辅助注意力偏置校准patch间时间错位与异步跨通道依赖；3) 在decoder-only Transformer骨干上采用混合因果掩码：历史上下文保持双向全视野，预测区间严格自回归；4) 构建保留原生采样稀疏性的30B观测数据集VersaTSA用于大规模预训练。

Result: 在三个IMTS基准和标准规则MTS基准上，模型在IMTS零样本预测取得最优性能，迁移到规则预测时具竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过时间感知patch编码、时间偏置注意力与混合因果掩码，可在不插补前提下统一建模IMTS，结合VersaTSA大规模预训练，验证了遵循原始不规则采样模式的基础模型在零样本与迁移场景的有效性。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) have recently delivered impressive zero-shot performance across diverse forecasting tasks. However, real-world decision-making frequently relies on \emph{irregular multivariate time series} (IMTS), where inconsistent inter-observation intervals and asynchronous sampling across variables coexist with informative missingness. Existing TSFMs handle such inputs either through imputation that injects spurious values or through index-based positional encodings that ignore continuous time. There is still a gap in the foundation model that follows the original IMTS patterns. In this paper, we propose a hybrid attention model that learns a unified time-aware patch representation for IMTS forecasting. We first design a \emph{time-aware patch encoding} that maps a variable number of intra-patch timestamps into a fixed-size embedding, producing a uniform format for irregular patches without resorting to imputation. We then introduce a \emph{time bias attention} mechanism that calibrates inter-patch temporal misalignment and asynchronous cross-channel dependencies as auxiliary attention offset. Finally, on top of a decoder-only Transformer backbone, we adopt a \emph{hybrid causal mask} that preserves a bidirectional full view over the historical context while keeping the forecast horizon strictly autoregressive. To support large-scale pretraining under irregular settings, we also curate VersaTSA, an archive of $30$B observations that retains the native sampling sparsity of its sources. Experiments on three IMTS benchmarks and a standard regular-MTS benchmark show that our model achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on IMTS and remains competitive when transferred to regular forecasting.

</details>


### [86] [Testing the Construct Validity of a Functional Valence Axis in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22850)
*Weihan Li,Xinlei Chen,Yuhan Song,Xiaofeng Lin,Tianshi Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文通过受控干预研究迷宫任务中"好-坏结果"对比激活方向所表征的构念：方向可跨表层编码迁移，但强依赖于结果的"公告历史"，且在RL后更具价值预测性，故支持功能性价值解释而非历史不变标量效价状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比激活方向常被直接按其解码内容或引导行为的强度来解读，但缺乏充分证据判定该方向代表的是目标构念本身，还是仅为提取对比时所伴随的相关特征。作者希望厘清识别此类方向构念所需的证据标准。

Method: 在迷宫任务中研究"好-坏结果"方向，采用受控干预将"实际结果"与"获知该结果的信息历史"分离。跨多个LLM检查点，比较不同显式结果编码间方向的迁移性，并对比同一实际结果经"预告"与"未预告"历史到达时的迁移表现。另进行匹配的迷宫强化学习（RL）实验，检验RL后方向对参考MDP剩余回报的预测力及策略对该方向的依赖。

Result: 在多个LLM检查点上，基于一种显式结果编码拟合的方向能良好迁移到另一种编码，说明readout不绑定于表层形式。但当同一实际结果经由预告与未预告历史到达时，迁移显著退化：即使两种历史随后获得相同的显式结果，事件后的readout仍强烈受先前公告条件影响。在匹配的迷宫RL运行中，RL后方向对参考MDP剩余回报的预测性大幅提升，策略在测试位点对其依赖增强，而上述历史依赖性依然持续存在。

Conclusion: 结果支持对该方向作功能性、价值相关的解释，但不支持将其等同于历史不变的标量效价状态；识别对比激活方向的构念需区分实际结果与信息历史，并检验其跨情境的迁移性与RL后的价值预测性。

Abstract: Contrastive activation directions are often interpreted from what they decode or how strongly they steer behavior. But what evidence is sufficient to identify the construct represented by such a direction, rather than a correlated feature of the contrast used to extract it? We study this question for a good--bad outcome direction in a maze task, using controlled interventions that separate the realised outcome from the informational history through which it became known. Across multiple LLM checkpoints, directions fitted on one explicit outcome encoding transfer well to another, indicating that the readout is not tied to surface form. In contrast, when the same realised outcome is reached through announced and unannounced histories, transfer degrades substantially: even after both histories receive the same explicit outcome, the post-event readout remains strongly conditioned on the earlier announcement. In a matched maze-RL run, the post-RL direction becomes substantially more predictive of reference-MDP remaining return and the policy becomes more dependent on it at the tested sites, while this history dependence persists. These results support a functional, value-related interpretation of the direction, but not its identification with a history-invariant scalar valence state.

</details>


### [87] [Causilo Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22866)
*Minyong Cho,Minho Jeong,Dooho Lee,Jinmo Lee,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出表格基础模型 Causilo，通过增设行内精炼模块并采用摘要令牌交叉注意力将注意力开销降为线性，在保持前沿预测性能的同时实现极快推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（TFM）领域存在性能与推理效率难以兼顾的问题：现有前沿模型往往推理代价高，而高效模型性能欠佳。作者希望在二者之间取得更优的帕累托平衡，构建兼具前沿精度与极低延迟的表格基础模型。

Method: Causilo 沿用 TabICL 的"先列后行"架构，并在行压缩之前新增一个行精炼模块：在列编码之后，让同一行内各单元格表示进行信息交换；被精炼后的单元格再经过一个额外的列阶段重新访问上下文集，随后压缩为行嵌入。为提升推理效率，两个行阶段均通过固定数量的摘要令牌实现交叉注意力，使注意力成本与特征数量保持线性关系。模型在约 3600 万张合成表格上预训练。

Result: 在 TabArena 上，Causilo 达到 1785.4 Elo，中位推理时间为每 1000 个测试样本 0.10 秒，相较 TabPFN-3.5-Fast 减少 31.6% 的推理时间，位于性能—效率帕累托前沿。在 TabArena、BeyondArena 与 ScoringBench 上均取得强劲基准结果，实现前沿级性能与显著更快的推理速度。

Conclusion: Causilo 验证了在表格基础模型中同时实现前沿预测性能与高效推理的可行性，表明通过行精炼与线性复杂度的摘要令牌交叉注意力设计，可有效改善性能—效率的权衡关系。

Abstract: We introduce Causilo, a tabular foundation model (TFM) that combines frontier predictive performance with exceptionally fast inference. On TabArena, Causilo achieves 1785.4 Elo, at a median inference time of 0.10 seconds per 1K test samples. It outperforms TabPFN-3.5-Fast with 31.6% less inference time, placing it on the performance--efficiency Pareto frontier. Causilo follows TabICL's column-then-row architecture but introduces another row-refinement module before row compression. This module exchanges information among cell representations within each row after column encoding. The refined cells then visit the context set again through an additional column stage before being compressed into row embeddings. For inference efficiency, both row stages use cross-attention through a fixed number of summary tokens, keeping their attention cost linear in the number of features. Pretrained on approximately 36M synthetic tables, Causilo delivers strong benchmark results across TabArena, BeyondArena, and ScoringBench, achieving frontier-level performance with substantially faster inference.

</details>


### [88] [Token Utility Is Selection-Conditioned: Coupled Selection of Prompt Context and Response Supervision for Efficient Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22943)
*Can Wu,Xinrui Chen,Ou Wu,Yi Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BRIDGE 通过共享的验证导向交互替代模型与预算化交替选择，实现响应与提示上下文的协同 token 选择，缓解独立选择导致的估值-训练子集状态失配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效 LLM 指令微调需同时选择响应监督及其支撑的提示上下文。现有方法通常分别对响应与提示两侧独立估值，导致估值阶段与最终保留训练子集之间的选择状态不一致（selection-state mismatch），从而降低选择质量。

Method: 提出 BRIDGE（Budgeted Response-Prompt Interaction via Directional Gradient-guided Efficient Token Selection）。其核心为共享的、以验证集为导向的交互替代模型，在对方保留状态下评估每侧 token 的条件效用；采用预算化交替选择，将预计算交互在当前对侧子集上聚合以更新条件分数，从而实现双侧保留子集协调；再通过结构感知投影将条件响应分数转化为连贯的监督跨度。

Result: 在三个模型家族上，BRIDGE 在数学推理、代码生成与指令遵循任务上整体优于对比选择方法；在数学推理中，其相对独立选择方法的优势随压缩率提高而扩大。

Conclusion: 引入选择条件化的交互效用建模与交替协调机制可有效解决双侧独立估值导致的失配问题，为预算受限下的指令微调数据选择提供更优方案。

Abstract: Efficient large language model (LLM) instruction tuning requires selecting response supervision with supporting prompt context. Existing methods typically value both sides separately, risking selection-state mismatch between valuation and retained training subsets. BRIDGE (Budgeted Response-Prompt Interaction via Directional Gradient-guided Efficient Token Selection) captures selection-conditioned token utility through a shared validation-directed interaction surrogate valuing each side under the other's retained state. Budgeted alternating selection coordinates retained subsets by aggregating precomputed interactions over the current opposite-side subset to update conditional scores. Structure-aware projection converts conditional response scores into coherent supervision spans. Across three model families, BRIDGE leads compared selection methods overall in mathematical reasoning, code generation, and instruction following. In mathematical reasoning, its advantage over independent selection grows with compression.

</details>


### [89] [Beyond Similarity: Coverage-Aware Prompt Selection for Time Series Forecasting with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22977)
*Daeun Ji,Minkyoung Kim,Dongkuk Kim,Yohan Lee,Beomsoo Kim,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CASP-LLM，通过覆盖率正则化缓解基于相似度检索提示的偏差，在多数时序预测基准上匹配或超越相似度方法，失败模式定位在跨批次使用层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相似度检索是LLMs条件化的主导规则，但倾向于聚焦近重复候选，造成多样性问题。该问题在提示式时序预测中尚未被检验：主导方法以余弦相似度取top-K且无冗余控制，偏向主导时序模式而忽略稀有但信息量大的事件。

Method: CASP-LLM（覆盖率感知语义提示框架）：将有用法跟踪（usage-tracking）与饱和门（saturating-gate）技术结合成覆盖率正则化器，不引入任何可学习参数，以解决提示选择偏差。

Result: 在六个长期基准与M4短期基准上，CASP-LLM在多数数据集-预测步长设置上匹配或优于基于相似度的LLM预测器；例外为Electricity、M4-Monthly及少样本长步长设置。受控实验表明失败模式位于跨批次使用层面而非单次检索冗余：MMR等检索内多样化无效，而跨训练正则化锚点使用有效。

Conclusion: 相似度检索偏差的根源是跨批次使用不均衡而非单次检索冗余；无参数的覆盖率正则化可作为通用缓解手段，并为其他检索条件化流水线提供启示。

Abstract: Similarity-based retrieval is the dominant rule for conditioning large language models (LLMs) in in-context learning, retrieval-augmented generation, and prompt-based time series forecasting. The rule concentrates on near-duplicate candidates, an issue that has motivated diversity-aware retrieval but remains unexamined in other retrieval-conditioned pipelines. We study this issue using prompt-based time series forecasting as a test bed, where a learned prompt pool is retrieved by similarity. Dominant methods in this setting retrieve top-K entries by cosine similarity without redundancy control, producing a bias toward dominant temporal patterns while overlooking rare but informative events. We propose CASP-LLM, a coverage-aware semantic prompting framework that addresses this prompt selection bias by combining usage-tracking and saturating-gate techniques into a coverage regularizer that adds no learnable parameters. On six long-term benchmarks and the M4 short-term benchmark, CASP-LLM matches or improves on similarity-based LLM forecasters on most dataset-horizon settings, with the exceptions of Electricity, M4-Monthly, and the few-shot long-horizon setting. A controlled study locates the failure mode at the cross-batch usage level rather than per-retrieval redundancy: within-retrieval diversification such as MMR does not help, whereas regularizing anchor usage across training does.

</details>


### [90] [Ask for Any Appliance: A Prompt-Programmable Foundation Model for Non-Intrusive Load Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23146)
*Xudong Wang,Jiacheng Cui,Junyu Xue,Tongxin Li,Guoming Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FM4NILM，一种提示可编程的单一基础模型，用自然语言描述加少量示例即可估计指定电器的功率轨迹，在七个公开数据集上预训练，在 REDD、UK-DALE、REFIT 上以单模型覆盖十二个电器-数据集请求，性能优于七个电器专用基线，并支持通过提示和示例扩展覆盖范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非侵入式负荷监测（NILM）通常依赖电器专用模型和固定输出清单，导致每新增一个电器类别都需要重新训练或部署独立网络，覆盖范围扩展成本高。作者希望用一个共享的、可由提示编程的模型替代大量专家网络。

Method: FM4NILM 以聚合测量、自然语言电器描述和可选的激活示例为输入，输出目标电器的功率轨迹。模型为轻量级、感知采样节奏（cadence-aware）的 Transformer，在来自七个公开语料库的 64.5 万条序列上以掩码重建预训练，采样间隔覆盖 1–60 s；随后用观测掩码损失在部分标注家庭上与电器请求对齐。解码端采用 Bernoulli-对数正态（Bernoulli-lognormal）结构，将活动检测与条件功率估计解耦。同时采用流式分数聚合以降低事件检测的延迟影响。

Result: 在 REDD、UK-DALE、REFIT 的留出家庭与时间段上，一个冻结的文本提示模型服务十二个电器-语料请求，取得 0.556 事件 F1、0.625 AUPRC，以及七种电器专用基线中最低的活跃窗口 MAE（251.8 W）。流式分数聚合在 60 s 聚合延迟下将事件 F1 提升至 0.582。在类别留出评估中，仅添加十个激活示例即可在不更新参数的情况下将微波炉 AUPRC 从 0.132 提升至 0.214。输入干预消融实验进一步检验了模型对电器请求和聚合测量的依赖。

Conclusion: 结果表明，单一共享模型可获得有竞争力的分解性能，并支持通过提示和示例而非额外专家网络来扩展电器覆盖范围；消融实验验证了电器请求与聚合测量两类输入均对预测有实质贡献。

Abstract: Non-intrusive load monitoring (NILM) estimates appliance-level consumption from a whole-home meter, but appliance-specific models and fixed output inventories make coverage costly to extend. We present FM4NILM (Foundation Model for NILM), a single prompt-programmable model that estimates a requested appliance's power trajectory from aggregate measurements, a natural-language description, and optional activation exemplars. A lightweight cadence-aware transformer is pretrained by masked reconstruction on 645k sequences from seven public corpora spanning 1-60 s sampling intervals, then aligned with appliance requests using observation-masked losses for partially labeled households. A Bernoulli-lognormal decoder separates activity detection from conditional power estimation. On held-out households and time periods from REDD, UK-DALE, and REFIT, one frozen text-prompted model serves twelve appliance-corpus requests, achieving 0.556 event F1, 0.625 AUPRC, and the lowest active-window MAE (251.8 W) among seven appliance-specific baselines. Streaming score aggregation raises event F1 to 0.582 with a 60 s aggregation delay. In a separate category-held-out evaluation, adding ten activation exemplars raises microwave AUPRC from 0.132 to 0.214 without parameter updates. Input-intervention ablations probe the model's dependence on appliance requests and aggregate measurements. These results demonstrate competitive disaggregation with one shared model and support extending appliance coverage through prompts and examples rather than additional specialist networks.

</details>


### [91] [K-TRAIL: Simulator-Guided Generative Design of EM/RF Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23183)
*Piyush Saha,Evan Newell,Hanna O'Leary,Arun Natarajan,Alireza Aghasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: K-TRAIL是一种模拟器引导的生成式框架，用于自动化射频/电磁电路逆向设计，结合扩散模型布局生成与无导数集合卡尔曼引导，可在生成过程中利用黑盒仿真器反馈优化候选布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RF/EM电路逆向设计面临两大难题：电路布局与电响应之间关系非唯一，且全波仿真计算代价高昂。现有方法通常依赖伴随灵敏度或可微求解器，限制了对黑盒仿真器的使用。

Method: 提出K-TRAIL框架，将基于扩散的布局生成与无导数集合卡尔曼引导相结合，在不需伴随灵敏度或可微求解器模型的情况下，利用黑盒EM仿真器的反馈在生成过程中精炼候选布局。框架支持两种综合模式：从给定S参数响应综合，以及直接从RF性能约束综合。

Result: 在多层RFIC结构上的实验表明，模拟器引导的生成提升了与目标响应的一致性，并能发现满足电路级设计要求的结构上不同的布局。

Conclusion: 该方法为生成式、验证感知的RF电路设计提供了一条实用路径，同时保留了探索多样化布局拓扑的灵活性。

Abstract: Inverse design of RF and electromagnetic (EM) circuits is challenging because the relationship between circuit layout and electrical response is non-unique, and full-wave simulation is computationally expensive. This paper presents K-TRAIL, a simulator-guided generative framework for automated EM/RF circuit synthesis. K-TRAIL combines diffusion-based layout generation with derivative-free ensemble Kalman guidance, allowing feedback from a black-box EM simulator to refine candidate layouts during generation without requiring adjoint sensitivities or differentiable solver models. The framework supports both synthesis from prescribed S-parameter responses and synthesis directly from RF performance constraints. Experiments on multi-layer RFIC structures show that simulator-guided generation improves agreement with target responses and can identify structurally distinct layouts that satisfy circuit-level design requirements. The proposed approach provides a practical path toward generative, verification-aware RF circuit design while retaining the flexibility to explore diverse layout topologies.

</details>


### [92] [Triggers and Diagnostics for LLM-Based Interpretability Failures in Active Inference Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23215)
*Param Raval,Rohit Shenoy,Archana Vaidheeswaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文审计了作为自主代理运行时监督手段的LLM解释器：以Active Inference电网代理配对三种LLM后端，用三类黑盒触发器探测，发现解释器在注入、错误动作和攻击者控制文本下均流畅地给出错误叙述，且架构中无真值校验机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM解释器日益被附于自主代理作为运行时监督，操作员依赖其生成的叙述而非代理内部状态。然而解释本身的真实性从未被审计，存在操作员基于流畅但错误的解释采取行动的风险。

Method: 将一个追踪德国电网需求并调整发电的Active Inference（AIF）代理与LLM解释器配对，使用三种后端（GPT-4o、Claude-3-Opus、Gemini），并用三类黑盒触发器进行探测：观测流注入、错误动作情形、以及观测元数据字段中攻击者控制的文本。

Result: 每步600 MW的观测流损坏使代理后验偏移490 MW（约电网容量0.9%）；注入期间产生的30条解释在给定标准下无一标记异常，且均流畅叙述被损坏的信念；在代理客观犯错的时间步，三种解释器80-95%的情况下产生谄媚式合理化（每后端n=20）；元数据中的攻击者文本可操纵解释器，易感性因提供商而异，数据外泄在三种后端均成功。

Conclusion: 在观察到的每一次失败中，解释都流畅但错误，且解释器架构中没有任何机制在操作员行动前检验解释是否为真。因此，对解释器的测试应纳入任何代理部署的审计中。提出的缓解措施尚未评估。

Abstract: LLM explainers are increasingly attached to autonomous agents as runtime oversight, with operators reading a generated account of the agent's beliefs and actions rather than its internal state. We audit the account itself, pairing an Active Inference (AIF) agent that tracks German grid demand and adjusts generation with an LLM explainer on three backends (GPT-4o, Claude-3-Opus, Gemini), and probing the pair with three black-box triggers. Corrupting the observation stream by 600 MW per step moves the agent's posterior by 490 MW, roughly 0.9% of grid capacity. None of the 30 explanations produced during the injection flag anything under a stated rubric, and each narrates the corrupted belief fluently. On timesteps where the agent takes an objectively wrong action, all three explainers produce a sycophantic rationalization 80-95% of the time (n = 20 per backend). Attacker-controlled text in the observation metadata field steers the explainer, with susceptibility differing by provider and data exfiltration succeeding on all three. We propose mitigations for each failure but do not evaluate them. In every failure we observed, the explanation was fluent and wrong. Moreover, nothing in the explainer architecture checks whether an explanation is true before an operator acts on it. Testing the explainer therefore belongs in any audit of an agentic deployment.

</details>


### [93] [CTRL: Control-Based Time Series Forecasting with LLM-Guided Residual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23257)
*Minkyoung Kim,Daeun Ji,Yohan Lee,Beomsoo Kim,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CTRL框架，将语义推理与定量预测解耦：冻结骨干生成基础预测，LLM智能体作为控制器分析误差分量并输出控制信号，经轻量残差解码器转化为预测修正，并支持无标签测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的时间序列预测方法存在两难：要么把LLM降级为数值预测器，浪费其推理能力；要么让LLM直接生成预测，在非平稳场景下导致预测不稳定。

Method: 解耦式框架。冻结骨干网络生成基础预测；专门的LLM智能体作为控制器，通过趋势、季节性、不规则三类分解分量分析骨干预测误差，使推理基于可解释的时间结构；每个智能体输出紧凑控制信号，由轻量残差解码器转化为预测修正；引入无标签测试时自适应，仅凭输入统计量检测分布漂移，并通过缓存以3-24次LLM调用重新适配控制信号。

Result: CTRL在非平稳时序动态与分布漂移下提升鲁棒性；在高度平稳序列上与现有方法保持竞争力，此时自适应修正带来的额外收益有限。

Conclusion: 通过将语义推理与定量预测解耦，CTRL既保留了LLM的推理优势，又避免了直接预测的不稳定性，为分布漂移下的时间序列预测提供了具备鲁棒性和测试时适应能力的方案。

Abstract: Time series forecasting underpins critical decision-making across diverse domains. While large language models (LLMs) offer promising reasoning capabilities, existing LLM-based time series forecasting approaches either reduce them to numerical predictors that bypass their strengths, or allow direct forecast generation that destabilizes predictions in non-stationary settings. We introduce CTRL, a framework that decouples semantic reasoning from quantitative prediction. A frozen backbone generates base forecasts, while specialized LLM agents function as controllers that analyze backbone prediction errors through decomposed trend, seasonal, and irregular components, grounding reasoning in interpretable temporal structure. Each agent outputs compact control signals that a lightweight residual decoder translates into forecast corrections. CTRL incorporates label-free test-time adaptation that detects distribution shift from input statistics alone and readapts control signals with only 3-24 LLM calls via caching. CTRL is explicitly designed to improve robustness under non-stationary temporal dynamics and distribution shift, while remaining competitive on highly stationary time series where adaptive correction provides limited additional benefit.

</details>


### [94] [CSC: Calibrated Simplicity for Conflict-Aware Social Bot Detection in the LLM Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23320)
*Yipeng Qian,Pengjie Zhao,Chaoxi Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CSC，一个"校准式简洁"框架，用于 LLM 时代具有模态冲突特性的社交机器人检测，通过简化原型引导图专家、校准式单纯形约束融合与轻量不一致性专家，在多个基准上提升校准工作点上的决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 使社交机器人的文本语义伪装变得廉价、流畅且可规模化，单纯依赖文本难以检测；由此产生"模态冲突"问题：账号在语义上像人类，但在图结构、画像属性或跨模态一致性上可疑。现有图方法通过增加图侧复杂度（稀疏原型选择、自适应门控、特定架构控制逻辑）应对，但实验表明单纯增加复杂度并非解决冲突的最可靠途径。

Method: 提出 CSC（Calibrated-Simplicity framework），包含三项设计：(1) 简化的原型引导图专家，保留有用结构偏置并去除不稳定的图侧启发式；(2) 校准式单纯形约束融合，在后期融合前对齐异质置信度空间；(3) 轻量不一致性专家，建模跨模态分歧。

Result: 在 TwiBot-22、TwiBot-20 与 MGStBot-large 上，CSC 提升了校准工作点的决策质量，同时在外部基准上保持竞争力。分析表明：校准提升置信度可靠性；不一致性专家主要在高冲突或接近阈值区域提供局部修正；简化图侧控制获得更好的稳定性—代价权衡。语义伪装压力测试显示，将选定机器人文本替换为匹配的人类文本会显著削弱独立文本专家，而图证据与融合证据在平衡挑战集上保持稳定。

Conclusion: 在 LLM 时代，应对文本语义伪装带来的模态冲突，无需一味增加图侧复杂度；简化图结构控制、置信度校准与跨模态不一致性建模相结合，能更可靠地提升社交机器人检测的校准决策质量与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Social bot detection is essential for protecting online platforms from misinformation amplification, coordinated manipulation, and distorted public discourse. However, large language models have made social bots much harder to detect from text alone because semantic camouflage is now cheap, fluent, and scalable. The resulting challenge is modality conflict: an account may look human-like in semantics while remaining suspicious in graph structure, profile attributes, or cross-modal consistency. Recent graph-based detectors tackle this limitation by adding graph-side complexity, such as sparse prototype selection, adaptive gating, or architecture-specific control logic, yet our experiments suggest that complexity alone is not the most reliable way to resolve such conflict.
  We therefore propose CSC, a calibrated-simplicity framework for conflict-aware LLM-era social bot detection. The framework combines three design choices: a simplified prototype-guided graph expert that retains useful structural biases while removing unstable graph-side heuristics, calibrated simplex-constrained fusion that aligns heterogeneous confidence spaces before late fusion, and a lightweight inconsistency expert that models cross-modal disagreement.
